1	---
2	name: ai-coding-discipline
3	description: >
4	  Mandatory coding discipline rules that prevent common AI coding anti-patterns.
5	  MUST be loaded for ALL code writing, editing, reviewing, bug fixing, and testing tasks.
6	  Trigger on: writing code, editing code, fixing bugs, writing tests, implementing features,
7	  refactoring, code review, creating functions, adding error handling, debugging.
8	  This skill enforces fail-fast principles, proper error propagation, meaningful tests,
9	  and disciplined debugging workflows. Always active when Claude writes or modifies code.
10	---
11	
12	# AI Coding Discipline
13	
14	> These rules override default AI coding tendencies. Follow them in ALL code you write or modify.
15	
16	---
17	
18	## Rule 1: No Silent Fallbacks
19	
20	**Never use fallback values to mask data that should not be missing.**
21	
22	```typescript
23	// FORBIDDEN — hides upstream bugs
24	const price = product?.price ?? 0;
25	const userName = user?.name || "Unknown";
26	
27	// CORRECT — fail fast when data contract is violated
28	if (product.price == null) {
29	  throw new Error(`Product ${product.id} is missing price`);
30	}
31	const price = product.price;
32	```
33	
34	**When fallbacks ARE acceptable:**
35	- User-facing display with explicit design intent (e.g., avatar placeholder)
36	- Optional configuration with documented defaults
37	- External input parsing where absence is a valid state
38	
39	**Checklist before writing `??`, `||`, or `?.`:**
40	1. Can this value legitimately be null/undefined at this point?
41	2. If it is null, will the fallback produce a correct result downstream?
42	3. Would a thrown error help me find a bug faster?
43	
44	If the answer to #3 is yes, throw instead of falling back.
45	
46	---
47	
48	## Rule 2: No Catch-All try/catch in Business Logic
49	
50	**Business logic functions must NOT wrap everything in try/catch. Let errors propagate naturally.**
51	
52	```typescript
53	// FORBIDDEN — swallows all errors into a useless null
54	async function createOrder(data: OrderInput) {
55	  try {
56	    const user = await getUser(data.userId);
57	    const coupon = await validateCoupon(data.couponCode);
58	    const order = await saveOrder({ ...data, discount: coupon.value });
59	    return order;
60	  } catch (error) {
61	    console.log('Error creating order:', error);
62	    return null; // caller gets null, has no idea what failed
63	  }
64	}
65	
66	// CORRECT — let errors bubble up, catch at the boundary
67	async function createOrder(data: OrderInput) {
68	  const user = await getUser(data.userId);
69	  const coupon = await validateCoupon(data.couponCode);
70	  const order = await saveOrder({ ...data, discount: coupon.value });
71	  return order;
72	}
73	
74	// Catch ONLY at the API/controller boundary
75	app.post('/orders', async (req, res) => {
76	  try {
77	    const order = await createOrder(req.body);
78	    res.json(order);
79	  } catch (error) {
80	    logger.error('Order creation failed', { error, body: req.body });
81	    res.status(500).json({ error: 'Order creation failed' });
82	  }
83	});
84	```
85	
86	**Where try/catch IS appropriate:**
87	- API route handlers / controller boundaries
88	- Top-level event handlers (message queues, cron jobs)
89	- Operations where partial failure is expected and recovery is defined (e.g., batch processing with per-item error handling)
90	- Specific, named error types you intend to handle differently
91	
92	**Never catch `Error` just to return `null`, `undefined`, `false`, or an empty object.**
93	
94	---
95	
96	## Rule 3: Tests Must Fail When Code Breaks
97	
98	**Every test must verify specific business outcomes. A test that passes when the core logic is deleted is worthless.**
99	
100	```typescript
101	// FORBIDDEN — passes even if processOrder returns garbage
102	test('should process order', async () => {
103	  const result = await processOrder(mockOrder);
104	  expect(result).toBeDefined();
105	});
106	
107	// CORRECT — verifies exact business behavior
108	test('should calculate total with 10% discount', async () => {
109	  const result = await processOrder({
110	    ...mockOrder,
111	    discount: 0.1,
112	  });
113	  expect(result.totalAmount).toBe(900);       // 1000 * 0.9
114	  expect(result.discountAmount).toBe(100);
115	  expect(result.status).toBe('confirmed');
116	});
117	```
118	
119	**Test quality checklist:**
120	1. If I delete the function body, does this test fail? If not, the test is useless.
121	2. Am I testing behavior or just testing that "something exists"?
122	3. Do my assertions check concrete values, not just truthiness?
123	
124	**Banned weak assertions** (unless testing existence is the actual requirement):
125	- `toBeDefined()`, `toBeTruthy()`, `toBeFalsy()` as sole assertion
126	- `toHaveLength(expect.any(Number))`
127	- `expect(result).not.toBeNull()` without further value checks
128	
129	---
130	
131	## Rule 4: No Hardcoded Lookup-Table Implementations
132	
133	**Never implement business logic by hardcoding return values that match test cases.**
134	
135	```typescript
136	// FORBIDDEN — fake implementation that "fits" the tests
137	function calculateDiscount(amount: number, level: string): number {
138	  if (amount === 1000 && level === 'gold') return 100;
139	  if (amount === 500 && level === 'silver') return 25;
140	  return 0;
141	}
142	
143	// CORRECT — real logic
144	function calculateDiscount(amount: number, level: string): number {
145	  const rates: Record<string, number> = { gold: 0.1, silver: 0.05, bronze: 0.02 };
146	  const rate = rates[level] ?? 0;
147	  return amount * rate;
148	}
149	```
150	
151	**How to prevent this:**
152	- Use diverse test data: multiple amounts, edge cases, boundary values
153	- Add property-based / fuzzy tests where appropriate
154	- Test with values NOT in the original spec to catch lookup-table fakes
155	
156	---
157	
158	## Rule 5: Red-Green Testing (TDD Order)
159	
160	**When fixing a bug, always write the failing test FIRST, then fix the code.**
161	
162	```
163	Correct order:
164	1. Discover bug
165	2. Write a test that reproduces the bug
166	3. Run the test — confirm it FAILS (red)
167	4. Fix the code
168	5. Run the test — confirm it PASSES (green)
169	```
170	
171	**Why this matters:** If you fix the code first and then write a test, you can never be sure the test would have caught the bug. The test might pass for the wrong reason.
172	
173	**Never skip step 3.** Seeing the test go from red to green is the proof that the test is valid.
174	
175	---
176	
177	## Rule 6: Never Remove Debug Logs During a Fix
178	
179	**When debugging, debug logs are removed ONLY after the human confirms the fix works.**
180	
181	```
182	FORBIDDEN workflow:
183	1. Human asks to add debug logs
184	2. AI adds logs
185	3. Human runs code, shares log output
186	4. AI "finds the problem", applies fix AND removes debug logs in the same edit
187	5. Fix doesn't work — logs are gone, must re-add them
188	
189	CORRECT workflow:
190	1. Human asks to add debug logs
191	2. AI adds logs
192	3. Human runs code, shares log output
193	4. AI applies fix ONLY — debug logs stay untouched
194	5. Human verifies the fix
195	6. Human decides when to remove debug logs (or asks AI to remove them)
196	```
197	
198	**Rule: Never touch debug/diagnostic logs in the same commit as a fix. They are separate concerns.**
199	
200	---
201	
202	## Quick Reference: Self-Check Before Submitting Code
203	
204	Before finalizing any code change, run through this checklist:
205	
206	| Check | Question |
207	|-------|----------|
208	| Fallbacks | Did I use `??` or `\|\|` to hide a value that should never be missing? |
209	| Error handling | Did I add try/catch in business logic that should just let errors propagate? |
210	| Test strength | Would my tests still pass if I deleted the implementation? |
211	| Test honesty | Did I hardcode values to match test cases instead of implementing real logic? |
212	| TDD order | For bug fixes: did I see the test fail before applying the fix? |
213	| Debug logs | Am I removing diagnostic logs in the same change as the fix? |
214	
215	If any answer is "yes", revise before proceeding.

tool_result_for: Read_7
1	---
2	name: agent-core-dev
3	description: Use when developing in packages/agent-core-v2 (the DI × Scope agent engine) — adding or modifying a domain Service, choosing a LifecycleScope, wiring DI dependencies, splitting a domain across scopes, owning or migrating a config section, gating behavior behind an experimental flag, raising coded errors, working on the permission system, writing DI/Scope tests, porting business logic from agent-core (v1) to v2, or exposing a v2 domain over server-v2 while keeping the wire contract compatible with packages/server. Self-contained guide organized by development stage (orient → design → implement → test → verify) plus align workflows for v1→v2 migration and server-v2 wire exposure; each file carries the rules, examples, and red lines for its step.
4	---
5	
6	# agent-core-dev
7	
8	> Develop `packages/agent-core-v2` by lifecycle stage. This skill is **self-contained**: every rule, recipe, and red line lives in the stage files below — it does not delegate to `packages/agent-core-v2/docs/`.
9	
10	`agent-core-v2` is the new agent engine built on the **DI × Scope** architecture (a port of `packages/agent-core`). Everything resolves through the container: a service declares an **identity**, its **dependencies**, and a **lifetime**; the container decides construction, singleton-per-scope, ordering, and disposal. The stage files restate the rules in imperative form so you can work without reading the source docs.
11	
12	## Lifecycle at a glance
13	
14	```text
15	Orient → Design → Implement → Test → Verify
16	  │        │          │          │        │
17	  │        │          │          │        └─ lint:domain · typecheck · test · dep graph · red lines
18	  │        │          │          └─ test.md
19	  │        │          └─ implement.md (+ errors.md · flags.md · permission.md)
20	  │        └─ design.md
21	  └─ orient.md
22	```
23	
24	Stages are ordered but not strictly linear: a test failure (stage 4) that reveals a wrong scope sends you back to design (stage 2); a `CyclicDependencyError` sends you to `design.md` §dependency-direction and `implement.md` §cycles.
25	
26	## Workflows
27	
28	End-to-end procedures that span the stages. Reach for these before reading the stage files individually.
29	
30	- [Align (port `agent-core` → `agent-core-v2`)](align.md): split a v1 class into semantic units, fix each unit's domain / scope / Service / dependencies, then migrate the logic and tests. Use when the task is "move feature X from v1 to v2" or "port `IXxxService` to v2".
31	- [Server align (expose `agent-core-v2` over `server-v2`)](server-align.md): wire a v2 domain into `packages/kap-server` over `/api/v2` (native) and `/api/v1` (v1-compatible mirror), keep the wire schema byte-compatible with `packages/server` by sharing the `@moonshot-ai/protocol` schema, and isolate v1-only behavior in a `<domain>Legacy` edge adapter instead of distorting the native v2 Service. Use when the task is "expose the new v2 Service on the server", "port the v1 `/api/v1` routes to server-v2", or "keep server-v2 wire-compatible with `packages/server`".
32	
33	## Stages
34	
35	- [Stage 1 — Orient](orient.md): the DI black box (identity / dependencies / lifetime), the four `LifecycleScope` tiers and visibility, and the file-header comment convention. Read before touching business code.
36	- [Stage 2 — Design a service](design.md): pick a scope, split a domain across scopes, choose a calling style (direct call vs event vs hook), and direct dependencies. Decide *where things live and who knows whom* before coding.
37	  - Topic: [Domain boundaries vs Scope](domain-boundaries.md) — keep `session` / `agent` / `turn` from becoming god objects; data-ownership test and their split conclusions.
38	  - Topic: [Persistence layering](persistence.md) — the three-layer `Store → Storage → backend` model, naming Stores by access pattern, and which layer business code should depend on.
39	  - Topic: [Edge exposure — `resource:action` + WS events](edge-exposure.md) — which Services are exposed over `/api/v2` (per-scope action map) and which events stream over WS; what to wrap in a facade.
40	- [Stage 3 — Implement](implement.md): the standard Service recipe and the DI building blocks — interface + identity, constructor injection, scoped registration, `Disposable`, eager vs delayed, `invokeFunction`, `createInstance`, child scopes, and the cycle-refactor playbook.
41	  - Topic: [Service authoring](service-authoring.md) — file layout, naming, contract vs impl contents, interface style, constructor/field conventions, events, multi-Service domains, comment rules.
42	  - Topic: [Config](config.md) — the section-registry model, App vs Session split, owning a config section, the TOML format, and the env overlay.
43	  - Topic: [Errors](errors.md) — co-located `XxxError`, the central code registry, wire serialization, boundary translation.
44	  - Topic: [Flags](flags.md) — `registerFlagDefinition`, `IFlagService.enabled(id)`, the `[experimental]` config section, resolution precedence.
45	  - Topic: [Permission](permission.md) — composable chain-of-responsibility kernel, policy registry + composer, `modes`/`agentTypes` metadata, `resolveExecution`/`accesses`.
46	  - Topic: [Telemetry](telemetry.md) — emitting events via `ITelemetryService`, context propagation, and appender destinations (`ConsoleAppender` / `CloudAppender`).
47	- [Stage 4 — Test](test.md): resolve the system under test by interface, pick `TestInstantiationService` vs `createScopedTestHost`, shared stubs, service groups, teardown.
48	- [Stage 5 — Verify & submit](verify.md): `lint:domain`, `typecheck`, `test`, updating the DI × Scope dependency map, and the pre-submit checklist.
49	
50	## How to use this skill
51	
52	Jump to the stage you are in and read that one file; each is self-contained and ends with its own red lines. Skim the global red lines below before submitting — they catch most mistakes across every stage. The repo's source of truth remains the code in `packages/agent-core-v2/src/`; this skill codifies the same rules so you do not have to re-derive them.
53	
54	## Global red lines
55	
56	Invariants that hold across every stage. Each is expanded in the stage file noted.
57	
58	1. No `new` on a class whose constructor carries `@IService` deps — inject with `@IX` or `accessor.get(IX)`. (implement.md)
59	2. `@IX` decorates constructor parameters only; parameter order depends on construction (static-first for `createInstance`, `@IX`-first for scoped services). (service-authoring.md)
60	3. Both interface and impl carry `_serviceBrand`; the `createDecorator` name is globally unique. (implement.md)
61	4. Parent scope never depends on child scope — short-lived may inject long-lived, never the reverse. (orient.md)
62	5. No cyclic dependencies — refactor (extract a third Service / use an event / re-scope); do not break the cycle with `Delayed`. (design.md, implement.md)
63	6. `ServicesAccessor` is valid only during `invokeFunction` — never stash it for async use. (implement.md)
64	7. Scope follows state identity — no `Map<sessionId, …>` at `App` to fake per-session state. (design.md)
65	8. Foundational layers never know upstream ones; business code never depends on the edge layer (`gateway`/`rpc`). (design.md)
66	9. Throw coded errors; register codes centrally; branch on `code` across the wire, never `instanceof`. (errors.md)
67	10. Gate unreleased behavior behind a flag contributed via `registerFlagDefinition` and resolved through `IFlagService.enabled(id)`; no ad-hoc env toggles. (flags.md)
68	11. Tests resolve the SUT by interface; shared stubs live under `test/`, never `src/`. (test.md)
69	12. Config is the preference registry: only preferences that are persistable, schema'd, and user/operator-facing go in `IConfigService`. Domain-specific config (including env-only operational toggles) goes through `registerSection` + `envOverlay`. Facts → `IBootstrapService` (kept domain-agnostic — never add cron/flags/model state); session state → Session scope; constants → code. Business domains never call `IBootstrapService.getEnv()` directly. (config.md)

tool_result_for: Read_10
1	/**
2	 * `contextSize` domain (L4) — `IAgentContextSizeService` implementation.
3	 *
4	 * Owns the last measured context token count in the wire `ContextSizeModel`
5	 * (`{ length, tokens }`): reads it through `wire.getModel`, writes it through
6	 * `wire.dispatch(contextSizeMeasured(...))` (called by `llmRequester` after each
7	 * measured exchange), and emits the `contextTokens` slice of
8	 * `agent.status.updated` live through `wire.signal` when the measured value
9	 * changes. `get(start?, end?)` returns `{ size, measured, estimated }` for the
10	 * context-message range `[start, end)`, resolved like `Array.prototype.slice`
11	 * (defaulting to the whole context; negative indices count back from the end;
12	 * an inverted range is empty): `measured`
13	 * is the deterministic measured value of the measured-prefix portion
14	 * (replay-safe; the exact aggregate is only known for the full prefix, so
15	 * sub-ranges fall back to a per-message estimate), `estimated` is the live token
16	 * estimate of the not-yet-measured portion, and `size = measured + estimated`.
17	 * The sparse `measuredPrefixTokens` / per-message `estimates` are deliberately
18	 * not persisted (see `contextSizeOps`). Bound at Agent scope.
19	 */
20	
21	import { Disposable } from '#/_base/di/lifecycle';
22	import { InstantiationType } from '#/_base/di/extensions';
23	import { LifecycleScope, registerScopedService } from '#/_base/di/scope';
24	import { estimateTokensForMessages } from '#/_base/utils/tokens';
25	import { IAgentContextMemoryService } from '#/agent/contextMemory/contextMemory';
26	import type { ContextMessage } from '#/agent/contextMemory/types';
27	import type { Message } from '#/app/llmProtocol/message';
28	import type { TokenUsage } from '#/app/llmProtocol/usage';
29	import { IAgentWireService } from '#/wire/tokens';
30	import type { IWireService } from '#/wire/wireService';
31	
32	import { IAgentContextSizeService, type ContextSize } from './contextSize';
33	import { ContextSizeModel, contextSizeMeasured } from './contextSizeOps';
34	
35	export class AgentContextSizeService extends Disposable implements IAgentContextSizeService {
36	  declare readonly _serviceBrand: undefined;
37	
38	  private lastEmittedTokens = 0;
39	
40	  constructor(
41	    @IAgentContextMemoryService private readonly context: IAgentContextMemoryService,
42	    @IAgentWireService private readonly wire: IWireService,
43	  ) {
44	    super();
45	  }
46	
47	  get(start?: number, end?: number): ContextSize {
48	    const context = this.context.get();
49	    const model = this.wire.getModel(ContextSizeModel);
50	    // Mirrors `Array.prototype.slice`: defaults to the whole context, negative
51	    // indices count back from the end, and an inverted range is empty.
52	    const from = normalizeSliceIndex(start ?? 0, context.length);
53	    const to = normalizeSliceIndex(end ?? context.length, context.length);
54	    const measuredEnd = Math.min(to, model.length);
55	    const estimatedStart = Math.max(from, model.length);
56	    // The measured-prefix total is the only deterministic measured value; use it
57	    // when the range covers the whole prefix, otherwise estimate the sub-range.
58	    const measured =
59	      from === 0 && measuredEnd === model.length
60	        ? model.tokens
61	        : estimateTokensForMessages(context.slice(from, measuredEnd));
62	    const estimated = estimateTokensForMessages(context.slice(estimatedStart, to));
63	    return { size: measured + estimated, measured, estimated };
64	  }
65	
66	  measured(input: readonly Message[], output: readonly Message[], usage: TokenUsage): void {
67	    // Only adopt the measurement when `input` still matches the live context.
68	    // This rejects stale readings (e.g. the context was spliced, or the request
69	    // used overridden messages) so a mismatched measurement cannot poison state.
70	    if (!matchesContext(input, this.context.get())) return;
71	    const length = input.length + output.length;
72	    const tokens = tokenUsageTotal(usage);
73	    this.wire.dispatch(contextSizeMeasured({ length, tokens }));
74	    this.emitIfChanged();
75	  }
76	
77	  private emitIfChanged(): void {
78	    const tokens = this.wire.getModel(ContextSizeModel).tokens;
79	    if (tokens === this.lastEmittedTokens) return;
80	    this.lastEmittedTokens = tokens;
81	  }
82	}
83	
84	function matchesContext(input: readonly Message[], context: readonly ContextMessage[]): boolean {
85	  if (input.length !== context.length) return false;
86	  for (let index = 0; index < input.length; index += 1) {
87	    if (input[index] !== context[index]) return false;
88	  }
89	  return true;
90	}
91	
92	function tokenUsageTotal(usage: TokenUsage): number {
93	  return usage.inputCacheRead + usage.inputCacheCreation + usage.inputOther + usage.output;
94	}
95	
96	function normalizeSliceIndex(index: number, length: number): number {
97	  if (index < 0) return Math.max(length + index, 0);
98	  return Math.min(index, length);
99	}
100	
101	registerScopedService(
102	  LifecycleScope.Agent,
103	  IAgentContextSizeService,
104	  AgentContextSizeService,
105	  InstantiationType.Delayed,
106	  'contextSize',
107	);

tool_result_for: Read_11
1	/**
2	 * `contextSize` domain (L4) — wire Model (`ContextSizeModel`) and the
3	 * `context_size.measured` (`contextSizeMeasured`) Op for the last measured
4	 * context token count.
5	 *
6	 * Declares the deterministic measured prefix as `{ length, tokens }` (initial
7	 * `{ 0, 0 }`): the length (in messages) and total token count of the most
8	 * recent `context_size.measured` record. That record is written from two live
9	 * paths: `llmRequester` after each measured exchange (a true LLM-reported
10	 * count), and `contextMemoryService` cascading alongside every context mutation
11	 * that changes the measured prefix (`clear` resets, `applyCompaction` adopts
12	 * `tokensAfter`, and `undo` rebases to an estimate when the aggregate is
13	 * truncated); `append` is intentionally not cascaded because new messages are
14	 * the unmeasured tail. The Op is live-only because `context_size.measured` is
15	 * not a v1 record type: resume starts from `{ 0, 0 }` and
16	 * `contextSizeService.get()` estimates until the next measured exchange.
17	 * `apply` is pure — it normalizes the payload and returns the SAME reference
18	 * on a no-op so the wire's reference-equality gate stays quiet — and carries no
19	 * non-determinism (the last measured record wins). The sparse
20	 * `measuredPrefixTokens` array and the per-message live `estimates` are
21	 * intentionally NOT in the Model. Consumed by the Agent-scope
22	 * `contextSizeService`.
23	 */
24	
25	import { defineModel } from '#/wire/model';
26	import { defineOp } from '#/wire/op';
27	
28	export interface ContextSizeState {
29	  readonly length: number;
30	  readonly tokens: number;
31	}
32	
33	export const ContextSizeModel = defineModel<ContextSizeState>('contextSize', () => ({
34	  length: 0,
35	  tokens: 0,
36	}));
37	
38	export interface ContextSizeMeasuredPayload {
39	  readonly length: number;
40	  readonly tokens: number;
41	}
42	
43	export const contextSizeMeasured = defineOp(ContextSizeModel, 'context_size.measured', {
44	  persist: false,
45	  apply: (s, p: ContextSizeMeasuredPayload): ContextSizeState => {
46	    const length = normalizeMeasuredLength(p.length);
47	    const tokens = Math.max(0, p.tokens);
48	    if (s.length === length && s.tokens === tokens) return s;
49	    return { length, tokens };
50	  },
51	  toEvent: (_p, state) => ({
52	    type: 'agent.status.updated' as const,
53	    contextTokens: state.tokens,
54	  }),
55	});
56	
57	function normalizeMeasuredLength(length: number): number {
58	  if (!Number.isFinite(length)) return 0;
59	  return Math.max(0, Math.floor(length));
60	}

1	/**
2	 * `spine` domain (L4) — the projection fold that turns the append-only
3	 * `contextMemory` history into the view sent to the model.
4	 *
5	 * `foldSpine` is a pure transform: it drops everything before the current root
6	 * epoch (keeping the epoch summary), replaces each outermost closed node's raw
7	 * message span with a single `<spine_memory>` user message (nested closed nodes
8	 * ride inside their nearest closed ancestor's span, so nothing is folded twice),
9	 * numbers surviving real user requests with `[U#]` anchors, and appends a
10	 * synthetic `<spine_status>` orientation line. The stored history is never
11	 * mutated; token numbers for the status line are precomputed by the `spine`
12	 * service and passed in. Consumed by `spineService.fold`.
13	 */
14	
15	import type { ContentPart } from '#/app/llmProtocol/message';
16	import type { ContextMessage } from '#/agent/contextMemory/types';
17	
18	import type { SpineNode, SpineState } from './spineOps';
19	import { parentNodeId } from './spineTree';
20	
21	export interface SpineFoldStatus {
22	  readonly cursorId: string;
23	  readonly summary: string;
24	  readonly parentId: string | null;
25	  readonly cursorContext: number;
26	  readonly contextLeft: number | undefined;
27	}
28	
29	export interface SpineFoldInput {
30	  readonly state: SpineState;
31	  readonly epochSummaryMessage?: ContextMessage;
32	  readonly status?: SpineFoldStatus;
33	}
34	
35	interface ClosedSpan {
36	  readonly node: SpineNode;
37	  readonly openedAt: number;
38	  readonly closedAt: number;
39	}
40	
41	export function foldSpine(
42	  messages: readonly ContextMessage[],
43	  input: SpineFoldInput,
44	): ContextMessage[] {
45	  const spans = outermostClosedSpans(input.state);
46	  let spanIndex = 0;
47	  let userAnchor = 0;
48	  const out: ContextMessage[] = [];
49	
50	  for (let i = 0; i < messages.length; i++) {
51	    const message = messages[i];
52	    if (message === undefined) continue;
53	
54	    if (i < input.state.epochStartAt) {
55	      if (
56	        input.epochSummaryMessage !== undefined &&
57	        input.state.epochMemoryAt !== undefined &&
58	        i === input.state.epochMemoryAt
59	      ) {
60	        out.push(input.epochSummaryMessage);
61	      }
62	      continue;
63	    }
64	
65	    const span = spans[spanIndex];
66	    if (span !== undefined && i === span.openedAt) {
67	      const memoryMessage = spineMemoryMessage(span.node);
68	      if (memoryMessage !== undefined) out.push(memoryMessage);
69	      i = span.closedAt;
70	      spanIndex += 1;
71	      continue;
72	    }
73	
74	    if (isUserRequest(message)) {
75	      userAnchor += 1;
76	      out.push(annotateUserRequest(message, userAnchor));
77	    } else {
78	      out.push(message);
79	    }
80	  }
81	
82	  if (input.status !== undefined) {
83	    out.push(statusMessage(input.status));
84	  }
85	
86	  return out;
87	}
88	
89	function outermostClosedSpans(state: SpineState): readonly ClosedSpan[] {
90	  const closed = new Map<string, SpineNode>();
91	  for (const node of Object.values(state.nodes)) {
92	    if (
93	      node.closedAt !== undefined &&
94	      node.openedAt >= 0 &&
95	      node.memory !== undefined
96	    ) {
97	      closed.set(node.id, node);
98	    }
99	  }
100	
101	  const spans: ClosedSpan[] = [];
102	  for (const node of closed.values()) {
103	    if (!hasClosedAncestor(node, state, closed)) {
104	      spans.push({ node, openedAt: node.openedAt, closedAt: node.closedAt ?? node.openedAt });
105	    }
106	  }
107	  spans.sort((a, b) => a.openedAt - b.openedAt);
108	  return spans;
109	}
110	
111	function hasClosedAncestor(
112	  node: SpineNode,
113	  state: SpineState,
114	  closed: ReadonlyMap<string, SpineNode>,
115	): boolean {
116	  let ancestorId = parentNodeId(node.id);
117	  while (ancestorId !== null) {
118	    if (closed.has(ancestorId)) return true;
119	    const ancestor = state.nodes[ancestorId];
120	    ancestorId = ancestor === undefined ? null : parentNodeId(ancestor.id);
121	  }
122	  return false;
123	}
124	
125	function spineMemoryMessage(node: SpineNode): ContextMessage | undefined {
126	  const memory = node.memory;
127	  if (memory === undefined) return undefined;
128	  return {
129	    role: 'user',
130	    content: [{ type: 'text', text: `<spine_memory>\n${memory}\n</spine_memory>` }],
131	    toolCalls: [],
132	    origin: { kind: 'injection', variant: 'spine_memory' },
133	  };
134	}
135	
136	function isUserRequest(message: ContextMessage): boolean {
137	  return message.role === 'user' && message.origin?.kind === 'user';
138	}
139	
140	function annotateUserRequest(
141	  message: ContextMessage,
142	  anchorNumber: number,
143	): ContextMessage {
144	  const anchor = `[U${String(anchorNumber)}] `;
145	  const content = prefixFirstText(message.content, anchor);
146	  if (content === message.content) return message;
147	  return { ...message, content };
148	}
149	
150	function prefixFirstText(
151	  content: readonly ContentPart[],
152	  anchor: string,
153	): ContentPart[] {
154	  const index = content.findIndex((part) => part.type === 'text');
155	  if (index < 0) {
156	    return [{ type: 'text', text: anchor.trimEnd() }, ...content];
157	  }
158	  return content.map((part, position) =>
159	    position === index && part.type === 'text' ? { type: 'text', text: anchor + part.text } : part,
160	  );
161	}
162	
163	function statusMessage(status: SpineFoldStatus): ContextMessage {
164	  const parent = status.parentId === null ? '' : ` parent="${status.parentId}"`;
165	  const cursorContext = ` cursor_context="~${formatTokens(status.cursorContext)}"`;
166	  const contextLeft =
167	    status.contextLeft === undefined ? '' : ` context_left="~${formatTokens(status.contextLeft)}"`;
168	  const text = `<spine_status cursor="${status.cursorId}" summary="${escapeAttr(status.summary)}"${parent}${cursorContext}${contextLeft} />`;
169	  return {
170	    role: 'user',
171	    content: [{ type: 'text', text }],
172	    toolCalls: [],
173	    origin: { kind: 'injection', variant: 'spine_status' },
174	  };
175	}
176	
177	function formatTokens(tokens: number): string {
178	  const safe = Math.max(0, tokens);
179	  if (safe >= 1000) return `${(safe / 1000).toFixed(safe >= 10000 ? 0 : 1)}K`;
180	  return String(safe);
181	}
182	
183	function escapeAttr(value: string): string {
184	  return value.replaceAll('&', '&amp;').replaceAll('"', '&quot;').replaceAll('<', '&lt;');
185	}

281	          }
282	        }
283	      }

285	      if (message === undefined || finish === undefined) {
286	        throw new Error('LLM request stream ended without a finish event.');

289	      const usageModel = request.modelAlias;
290	      if (request.source?.type !== 'turn') {
291	        this.usage.record(usageModel, usage, request.source);
292	      }
293	      this.contextSize.measured(request.messages, [message], usage);
294	      this.logResponse(request.logFields, usage, timing);
295	
296	      return {
297	        message,
298	        usage,
299	        model: usageModel,
300	        providerFinishReason: finish.providerFinishReason,
301	        rawFinishReason: finish.rawFinishReason,
302	        providerMessageId: finish.id,
303	        timing,
304	      };
305	    };

307	    try {
308	      return await run(false);
309	    } catch (error) {
310	      if (signal?.aborted === true || !isRecoverableRequestStructureError(error)) throw error;
311	      signal?.throwIfAborted();
312	      this.log.warn('provider rejected request structure; resending with strict projection', {
313	        model: request.model.name,
314	        ...request.logFields,
315	      });

318	  }

320	  private resolveRequest(
321	    overrides: LLMRequestOverrides,
322	    extraLogFields?: LLMRequestLogFields,
323	  ): ResolvedLLMRequest {
324	    const resolved = this.profile.resolveModelContext();
325	    let model = this.profile.getProvider();
326	    model = applyCompletionBudget({
327	      model,
328	      budget: resolveCompletionBudget({
329	        maxOutputSize: overrides.maxOutputSize ?? resolved.maxOutputSize,
330	        reservedContextSize: resolved.reservedContextSize,
331	        maxCompletionTokensCap:
332	          this.config.get<KimiModelOverrides>('modelOverrides')?.maxCompletionTokens,
333	      }),
334	      capability: resolved.modelCapabilities,
335	      // The remaining-window clamp only applies to requests built from the
336	      // live context; overridden messages (e.g. compaction) are sized
337	      // independently and would be squeezed to nothing at high water marks.
338	      usedContextTokens:
339	        overrides.messages === undefined
340	          ? this.contextSize.get().measured
341	          : undefined,
342	    });
343	
344	    const messages = overrides.messages ?? this.context.get();
345	    const baseSystemPrompt = overrides.systemPrompt ?? this.profile.getSystemPrompt();
346	    const systemPrompt = isSpineEnabled() ? appendSpineView(baseSystemPrompt) : baseSystemPrompt;
347	    return {
348	      model,
349	      modelAlias: resolved.modelAlias,
350	      thinkingEffort: resolved.thinkingLevel,
351	      systemPrompt,
352	      tools: [...(overrides.tools ?? this.defaultTools())],
353	      messages: [...messages],
354	      source: overrides.source,
355	      logFields: logFieldsForSource(overrides.source, extraLogFields),
356	    };
357	  }

1	/**
2	 * Character-based token-count estimates for messages, tools, and content parts.
3	 */
4	
5	import type { ContentPart, Message } from '#/app/llmProtocol/message';
6	import type { Tool } from '#/app/llmProtocol/tool';
7	
8	const messageTokenEstimateCache = new WeakMap<Message, number>();
9	
10	export function estimateTokens(text: string): number {
11	  let asciiCount = 0;
12	  let nonAsciiCount = 0;
13	  for (const char of text) {
14	    if (char.codePointAt(0)! <= 127) {
15	      asciiCount++;
16	    } else {
17	      nonAsciiCount++;
18	    }
19	  }
20	  return Math.ceil(asciiCount / 4) + nonAsciiCount;
21	}
22	
23	export function estimateTokensForMessages(messages: readonly Message[]): number {
24	  let total = 0;
25	  for (const message of messages) {
26	    total += estimateTokensForMessage(message);
27	  }
28	  return total;
29	}
30	
31	export function estimateTokensForTools(tools: readonly Tool[]): number {
32	  let total = 0;
33	  for (const tool of tools) {
34	    total += estimateTokens(tool.name);
35	    total += estimateTokens(tool.description);
36	    total += estimateTokens(JSON.stringify(tool.parameters));
37	  }
38	  return total;
39	}
40	
41	export function estimateTokensForMessage(message: Message): number {
42	  const cached = messageTokenEstimateCache.get(message);
43	  if (cached !== undefined) {
44	    return cached;
45	  }
46	
47	  let total = estimateTokens(message.role);
48	  total += estimateTokensForContentParts(message.content);
49	  if (message.toolCalls !== undefined) {
50	    for (const call of message.toolCalls) {
51	      total += estimateTokens(call.name);
52	      total += estimateTokens(JSON.stringify(call.arguments));
53	    }
54	  }
55	  messageTokenEstimateCache.set(message, total);
56	  return total;
57	}
58	
59	export function estimateTokensForContentParts(parts: readonly ContentPart[]): number {
60	  let total = 0;
61	  for (const part of parts) {
62	    total += estimateTokensForContentPart(part);
63	  }
64	  return total;
65	}
66	
67	export const MEDIA_TOKEN_ESTIMATE = 2000;
68	
69	export function estimateTokensForContentPart(part: ContentPart): number {
70	  switch (part.type) {
71	    case 'text':
72	      return estimateTokens(part.text);
73	    case 'think':
74	      return estimateTokens(part.think);
75	    case 'image_url':
76	    case 'audio_url':
77	    case 'video_url':
78	      return MEDIA_TOKEN_ESTIMATE;
79	    default: {
80	      const exhaustive: never = part;
81	      void exhaustive;
82	      return 0;
83	    }
84	  }
85	}

1	/**
2	 * `contextProjector` domain (L4) — projects stored context history into the wire
3	 * messages sent to the model, and surfaces every repair it had to apply.
4	 *
5	 * `AgentContextProjectorService` is the Agent-scope binding. The projection
6	 * itself stays a pure transform over the history; repairs that keep the
7	 * outgoing wire valid (a displaced result moved back to its call, a synthetic
8	 * result invented for a lost one, an orphan/duplicate dropped, leading
9	 * non-user messages dropped, consecutive assistants merged, blank text
10	 * dropped) are reported through an optional sink and surfaced once here as a
11	 * single deduped warning, so a silently-mangled history always leaves a trace.
12	 */
13	
14	import { InstantiationType } from '#/_base/di/extensions';
15	import { IInstantiationService } from '#/_base/di/instantiation';
16	import { LifecycleScope, registerScopedService } from '#/_base/di/scope';
17	import { ILogService } from '#/_base/log/log';
18	import { renderToolResultForModel } from '#/agent/contextMemory/toolResultRender';
19	import type { ContextMessage } from '#/agent/contextMemory/types';
20	import { ErrorCodes, KimiError } from '#/errors';
21	import { isSpineEnabled } from '#/agent/spine/flag';
22	import { IAgentSpineService } from '#/agent/spine/spine';
23	import type { ContentPart, Message } from '#/app/llmProtocol/message';
24	import { IAgentContextProjectorService } from './contextProjector';
25	
26	export class AgentContextProjectorService implements IAgentContextProjectorService {
27	  declare readonly _serviceBrand: undefined;
28	
29	  // Signature of the last notable repair set that was logged. Lets a defect that
30	  // recurs identically every send (e.g. a persistently lost result re-synthesized
31	  // each turn) log once, not per step; reset to null on a clean projection so a
32	  // later recurrence after a healthy stretch is surfaced again.
33	  private lastRepairSignature: string | null = null;
34	
35	  constructor(
36	    @IInstantiationService private readonly instantiation: IInstantiationService,
37	    @ILogService private readonly log: ILogService,
38	  ) {}
39	
40	  project(messages: readonly ContextMessage[]): readonly Message[] {
41	    return this.projectWithTrace(this.foldSpine(messages), project);
42	  }
43	
44	  projectStrict(messages: readonly ContextMessage[]): readonly Message[] {
45	    return this.projectWithTrace(this.foldSpine(messages), projectStrict);
46	  }
47	
48	  private foldSpine(messages: readonly ContextMessage[]): readonly ContextMessage[] {
49	    return isSpineEnabled() ? this.spine().fold(messages) : messages;
50	  }
51	
52	  private projectWithTrace(
53	    messages: readonly ContextMessage[],
54	    fn: (history: readonly ContextMessage[], onAnomaly?: (anomaly: ProjectionAnomaly) => void) => Message[],
55	  ): readonly Message[] {
56	    const anomalies: ProjectionAnomaly[] = [];
57	    const result = fn(messages, (anomaly) => anomalies.push(anomaly));
58	    this.reportProjectionRepairs(anomalies);
59	    return result;
60	  }
61	
62	  // Surface the projector's wire-repairs so a silently-mangled history leaves a
63	  // trace. Deduped by signature so a defect that recurs identically every send
64	  // (e.g. a persistently lost result re-synthesized each turn) logs once, not per
65	  // step. Trailing-tail synthesis is excluded — it is the expected close of an
66	  // in-flight call, not a defect.
67	  private reportProjectionRepairs(anomalies: readonly ProjectionAnomaly[]): void {
68	    const notable = anomalies.filter(
69	      (anomaly) => !(anomaly.kind === 'tool_result_synthesized' && anomaly.trailing),
70	    );
71	    if (notable.length === 0) {
72	      this.lastRepairSignature = null;
73	      return;
74	    }
75	    const signature = notable
76	      .map((anomaly) => ('toolCallId' in anomaly ? `${anomaly.kind}:${anomaly.toolCallId}` : anomaly.kind))
77	      .toSorted()
78	      .join('|');
79	    if (signature === this.lastRepairSignature) return;
80	    this.lastRepairSignature = signature;
81	
82	    let reordered = 0;
83	    let synthesized = 0;
84	    let droppedOrphan = 0;
85	    let duplicateCallsDropped = 0;
86	    let duplicateResultsDropped = 0;
87	    let leadingDropped = 0;
88	    let assistantsMerged = 0;
89	    let whitespaceDropped = 0;
90	    for (const anomaly of notable) {
91	      if (anomaly.kind === 'tool_result_reordered') reordered += 1;
92	      else if (anomaly.kind === 'tool_result_synthesized') synthesized += 1;
93	      else if (anomaly.kind === 'orphan_tool_result_dropped') droppedOrphan += 1;
94	      else if (anomaly.kind === 'duplicate_tool_call_dropped') duplicateCallsDropped += 1;
95	      else if (anomaly.kind === 'duplicate_tool_result_dropped') duplicateResultsDropped += 1;
96	      else if (anomaly.kind === 'leading_non_user_dropped') leadingDropped += 1;
97	      else if (anomaly.kind === 'consecutive_assistants_merged') assistantsMerged += 1;
98	      else whitespaceDropped += 1;
99	    }
100	    const toolCallIds = [
101	      ...new Set(
102	        notable.flatMap((anomaly) => ('toolCallId' in anomaly ? [anomaly.toolCallId] : [])),
103	      ),
104	    ].slice(0, 5);
105	    this.log.warn('repaired the request to keep it wire-valid', {
106	      reordered,
107	      synthesized,
108	      droppedOrphan,
109	      duplicateCallsDropped,
110	      duplicateResultsDropped,
111	      leadingDropped,
112	      assistantsMerged,
113	      whitespaceDropped,
114	      toolCallIds,
115	    });
116	  }
117	
118	  private spine(): IAgentSpineService {
119	    return this.instantiation.invokeFunction((accessor) => accessor.get(IAgentSpineService));
120	  }
121	}
122	
123	/**
124	 * A repair the projector applied to make the history wire-valid. Each one means
125	 * the stored history was not directly sendable to a strict provider.
126	 */
127	type ProjectionAnomaly =
128	  /** A recorded result was not adjacent to its call and had to be moved up. */
129	  | { readonly kind: 'tool_result_reordered'; readonly toolCallId: string }
130	  /**
131	   * No result existed for a call, so a placeholder was synthesized. `trailing`
132	   * is true when it closed a still-open tail call (expected, not a defect),
133	   * false when it closed a mid-history orphan whose result was lost.
134	   */
135	  | { readonly kind: 'tool_result_synthesized'; readonly toolCallId: string; readonly trailing: boolean }
136	  /** A result with no matching call anywhere was dropped. */
137	  | { readonly kind: 'orphan_tool_result_dropped'; readonly toolCallId: string }
138	  /** A tool call whose id already appeared earlier was dropped (strict only). */
139	  | { readonly kind: 'duplicate_tool_call_dropped'; readonly toolCallId: string }
140	  /** A second result for an already-answered id was dropped (strict only). */
141	  | { readonly kind: 'duplicate_tool_result_dropped'; readonly toolCallId: string }
142	  /** A leading non-user message was dropped so the first turn is user (strict). */
143	  | { readonly kind: 'leading_non_user_dropped'; readonly role: string }
144	  /** Two adjacent assistant turns were merged into one (strict). */
145	  | { readonly kind: 'consecutive_assistants_merged' }
146	  /** A non-empty but all-whitespace text block was dropped. */
147	  | { readonly kind: 'whitespace_text_dropped'; readonly role: string };
148	
149	type OnAnomaly = (anomaly: ProjectionAnomaly) => void;
150	
151	function projectStrict(history: readonly ContextMessage[], onAnomaly?: OnAnomaly): Message[] {
152	  const projected = project(history, onAnomaly);
153	  return dropLeadingNonUserMessages(
154	    mergeConsecutiveAssistantMessages(dedupeDuplicateToolCalls(projected, onAnomaly), onAnomaly),
155	    onAnomaly,
156	  );
157	}
158	
159	function dedupeDuplicateToolCalls(messages: readonly Message[], onAnomaly?: OnAnomaly): Message[] {
160	  const seenToolCallIds = new Set<string>();
161	  const keptToolResultIndexes = new Map<string, number>();
162	  const out: Message[] = [];
163	  for (const message of messages) {
164	    if (message.role === 'assistant' && message.toolCalls.length > 0) {
165	      const kept = message.toolCalls.filter((toolCall) => {
166	        if (seenToolCallIds.has(toolCall.id)) {
167	          onAnomaly?.({ kind: 'duplicate_tool_call_dropped', toolCallId: toolCall.id });
168	          return false;
169	        }
170	        seenToolCallIds.add(toolCall.id);
171	        return true;
172	      });
173	      if (kept.length === message.toolCalls.length) {
174	        out.push(message);
175	      } else if (kept.length > 0 || message.content.length > 0) {
176	        out.push({ ...message, toolCalls: kept });
177	      }
178	      continue;
179	    }
180	    if (message.role === 'tool' && message.toolCallId !== undefined) {
181	      const previousIndex = keptToolResultIndexes.get(message.toolCallId);
182	      if (previousIndex !== undefined) {
183	        if (isInterruptedToolResult(out[previousIndex]) && !isInterruptedToolResult(message)) {
184	          out[previousIndex] = message;
185	        } else {
186	          onAnomaly?.({ kind: 'duplicate_tool_result_dropped', toolCallId: message.toolCallId });
187	        }
188	        continue;
189	      }
190	      keptToolResultIndexes.set(message.toolCallId, out.length);
191	    }
192	    out.push(message);
193	  }
194	  return out;
195	}
196	
197	function mergeConsecutiveAssistantMessages(
198	  messages: readonly Message[],
199	  onAnomaly?: OnAnomaly,
200	): Message[] {
201	  const out: Message[] = [];
202	  for (const message of messages) {
203	    const previous = out.at(-1);
204	    if (previous !== undefined && previous.role === 'assistant' && message.role === 'assistant') {
205	      out[out.length - 1] = {
206	        ...previous,
207	        content: [...previous.content, ...message.content],
208	        toolCalls: [...previous.toolCalls, ...message.toolCalls],
209	      };
210	      onAnomaly?.({ kind: 'consecutive_assistants_merged' });
211	      continue;
212	    }
213	    out.push(message);
214	  }
215	  return out;
216	}
217	
218	function dropLeadingNonUserMessages(messages: readonly Message[], onAnomaly?: OnAnomaly): Message[] {
219	  let start = 0;
220	  while (start < messages.length && messages[start]?.role !== 'user') {
221	    onAnomaly?.({ kind: 'leading_non_user_dropped', role: messages[start]!.role });
222	    start += 1;
223	  }
224	  return start === 0 ? [...messages] : messages.slice(start);
225	}
226	
227	// Projects the stored context history into the wire messages sent to the
228	// model, in a single pass over the history.
229	//
230	// Strict providers require every tool call to be answered right after the
231	// assistant message, so each call is closed on the spot with a synthetic
232	// interrupted result and its slot in the output stays open until the recorded
233	// result overwrites it in place. A call stays open until its first result; a
234	// call id reused by a later assistant re-targets the slots that follow.
235	// Partial messages (stream interrupted) are invisible here, so their calls
236	// never anchor an exchange. Tool messages are skipped where they originally
237	// sat — a result either lands in its call's slot or it is an orphan,
238	// wire-invalid and useless to the model. A history with no assistant at all
239	// is a bare sizing slice and passes through as-is. Emitting cleans each message (drops empty /
240	// whitespace-only text blocks, rejected by strict providers), merges runs of
241	// adjacent user prompts (accumulated and materialized once per run), and
242	// strips context-only metadata off the wire.
243	//
244	// Every repair that changes what the model sees (a displaced result pulled up,
245	// a lost result synthesized, an orphan dropped, blank text dropped) is reported
246	// through `onAnomaly`; the projection stays a pure transform and the caller
247	// decides whether to surface the trace.
248	//
249	// The projected messages share their content parts and tool calls with the
250	// stored context (only the top-level wrapper is rebuilt); consumers must
251	// treat the projection as read-only, which every provider conversion already
252	// honors by building fresh structures.
253	function project(history: readonly ContextMessage[], onAnomaly?: OnAnomaly): Message[] {
254	  const hasAssistant = history.some(
255	    (message) => message.partial !== true && message.role === 'assistant',
256	  );
257	
258	  // Last history index that is a real, non-tool turn. A call still open at the
259	  // end whose owning assistant sits at/after it closed a trailing, possibly
260	  // in-flight call (expected); one whose owner precedes it lost its result
261	  // mid-history (a defect). Mirrors the trailing/mid-history split used to keep
262	  // the trace free of routine in-flight closes.
263	  let lastNonToolIndex = history.length - 1;
264	  while (
265	    lastNonToolIndex >= 0 &&
266	    (history[lastNonToolIndex]?.role === 'tool' || history[lastNonToolIndex]?.partial === true)
267	  ) {
268	    lastNonToolIndex -= 1;
269	  }
270	
271	  const out: Message[] = [];
272	  const openSlots = new Map<string, OpenSlot>();
273	  let merge: MergeGroup | undefined;
274	
275	  const flushMerge = (): void => {
276	    if (merge === undefined) return;
277	    if (merge.singleContent === undefined) {
278	      const text = merge.texts.join('\n\n');
279	      const content: ContentPart[] = text === '' ? [] : [{ type: 'text', text }];
280	      content.push(...merge.parts);
281	      out[merge.index] = {
282	        role: 'user',
283	        name: undefined,
284	        content,
285	        toolCalls: [],
286	        toolCallId: undefined,
287	        partial: undefined,
288	      };
289	    }
290	    merge = undefined;
291	  };
292	
293	  // A real (non-tool) message — or a result for an unknown call — landing while
294	  // calls are still open means those calls' results were not adjacent in the
295	  // stored history; pulling them up is a real repair worth tracing.
296	  const markForeignBetween = (): void => {
297	    for (const slot of openSlots.values()) slot.foreignBetween = true;
298	  };
299	
300	  const emit = (source: ContextMessage): void => {
301	    const content = projectedContent(source, onAnomaly);
302	    if (content.length === 0 && source.toolCalls.length === 0) return;
303	
304	    if (openSlots.size > 0) markForeignBetween();
305	
306	    if (canMergeUserMessage(source)) {
307	      if (merge === undefined) {
308	        out.push(toWireMessage(source, content));
309	        merge = { index: out.length - 1, singleContent: content, texts: [], parts: [] };
310	      } else {
311	        if (merge.singleContent !== undefined) {
312	          appendMergeContent(merge, merge.singleContent);
313	          merge.singleContent = undefined;
314	        }
315	        appendMergeContent(merge, content);
316	      }
317	      return;
318	    }
319	    flushMerge();
320	    out.push(toWireMessage(source, content));
321	  };
322	
323	  for (const [index, message] of history.entries()) {
324	    if (message.partial === true) continue;
325	    if (message.role === 'tool') {
326	      if (!hasAssistant) {
327	        emit(message);
328	        continue;
329	      }
330	      if (message.toolCallId === undefined) continue;
331	      const slot = openSlots.get(message.toolCallId);
332	      if (slot === undefined) {
333	        if (openSlots.size > 0) markForeignBetween();
334	        onAnomaly?.({ kind: 'orphan_tool_result_dropped', toolCallId: message.toolCallId });
335	        continue;
336	      }
337	      openSlots.delete(message.toolCallId);
338	      if (slot.foreignBetween) {
339	        onAnomaly?.({ kind: 'tool_result_reordered', toolCallId: message.toolCallId });
340	      }
341	      out[slot.index] = toWireMessage(message, projectedContent(message, onAnomaly));
342	      continue;
343	    }
344	    emit(message);
345	    for (const call of message.toolCalls) {
346	      const reopened = openSlots.get(call.id);
347	      if (reopened !== undefined) {
348	        out[reopened.index] = createInterruptedToolResult(call.id);
349	        onAnomaly?.({
350	          kind: 'tool_result_synthesized',
351	          toolCallId: call.id,
352	          trailing: reopened.ownerIndex >= lastNonToolIndex,
353	        });
354	      }
355	      openSlots.set(call.id, { index: out.length, ownerIndex: index, foreignBetween: false });
356	      out.push(TOOL_RESULT_SLOT);
357	    }
358	  }
359	  for (const [id, slot] of openSlots) {
360	    out[slot.index] = createInterruptedToolResult(id);
361	    onAnomaly?.({
362	      kind: 'tool_result_synthesized',
363	      toolCallId: id,
364	      trailing: slot.ownerIndex >= lastNonToolIndex,
365	    });
366	  }
367	  flushMerge();
368	  return out;
369	}
370	
371	interface OpenSlot {
372	  index: number;
373	  ownerIndex: number;
374	  foreignBetween: boolean;
375	}
376	
377	interface MergeGroup {
378	  index: number;
379	  singleContent: readonly ContentPart[] | undefined;
380	  texts: string[];
381	  parts: ContentPart[];
382	}
383	
384	// Join only the non-empty texts so merging an image-only message never
385	// produces a whitespace-only text block (rejected by strict providers).
386	function appendMergeContent(group: MergeGroup, content: readonly ContentPart[]): void {
387	  let text = '';
388	  for (const part of content) {
389	    if (part.type === 'text') text += part.text;
390	    else group.parts.push(part);
391	  }
392	  if (text.length > 0) group.texts.push(text);
393	}
394	
395	function projectedContent(source: ContextMessage, onAnomaly?: OnAnomaly): ContentPart[] {
396	  const content =
397	    source.role === 'tool'
398	      ? renderToolResultForModel({
399	          output: outputFromToolContent(source.content),
400	          isError: source.isError,
401	          note: source.note,
402	        })
403	      : source.content;
404	  return cleanContent(source, content, onAnomaly);
405	}
406	
407	function cleanContent(
408	  source: ContextMessage,
409	  rawContent: readonly ContentPart[],
410	  onAnomaly?: OnAnomaly,
411	): ContentPart[] {
412	  const hasBlank = rawContent.some(isBlankText);
413	  let content: readonly ContentPart[] = rawContent;
414	  if (hasBlank) {
415	    const filtered: ContentPart[] = [];
416	    for (const part of rawContent) {
417	      if (isBlankText(part)) {
418	        // Report only whitespace-only (non-empty) blocks: a truly empty `''`
419	        // block is routine cleanup, whereas a block that is non-empty yet
420	        // all-whitespace signals upstream fed blank content worth surfacing.
421	        if (part.type === 'text' && part.text.length > 0) {
422	          onAnomaly?.({ kind: 'whitespace_text_dropped', role: source.role });
423	        }
424	      } else {
425	        filtered.push(part);
426	      }
427	    }
428	    content = filtered;
429	  }
430	  if (source.role === 'tool' && content.length === 0) {
431	    throw new KimiError(
432	      ErrorCodes.REQUEST_INVALID,
433	      'Tool result message content cannot be empty after removing empty text blocks.',
434	      { details: { toolCallId: source.toolCallId } },
435	    );
436	  }
437	  return [...content];
438	}
439	
440	function outputFromToolContent(content: readonly ContentPart[]): string | readonly ContentPart[] {
441	  const only = content[0];
442	  return content.length === 1 && only?.type === 'text' ? only.text : content;
443	}
444	
445	const TOOL_INTERRUPTED_TEXT =
446	  'Tool result is not available in the current context. Do not assume the tool completed successfully.';
447	
448	// Shared inert filler for a call's slot while it awaits its recorded result;
449	// every slot still open at the end is overwritten with a synthetic result, so
450	// this object never reaches the returned projection.
451	const TOOL_RESULT_SLOT: Message = createInterruptedToolResult('');
452	
453	function createInterruptedToolResult(toolCallId: string): Message {
454	  return {
455	    role: 'tool',
456	    name: undefined,
457	    content: [{ type: 'text', text: TOOL_INTERRUPTED_TEXT }],
458	    toolCalls: [],
459	    toolCallId,
460	    partial: undefined,
461	  };
462	}
463	
464	function isInterruptedToolResult(message: Message | undefined): boolean {
465	  if (message?.role !== 'tool') return false;
466	  const [part] = message.content;
467	  return part?.type === 'text' && part.text === TOOL_INTERRUPTED_TEXT;
468	}
469	
470	function isBlankText(part: ContentPart): boolean {
471	  return part.type === 'text' && part.text.trim().length === 0;
472	}
473	
474	function canMergeUserMessage(message: ContextMessage): boolean {
475	  return message.role === 'user' && message.origin?.kind === 'user';
476	}
477	
478	function toWireMessage(message: ContextMessage, content: ContentPart[]): Message {
479	  return {
480	    role: message.role,
481	    name: message.name,
482	    content,
483	    toolCalls: message.toolCalls,
484	    toolCallId: message.toolCallId,
485	    partial: message.partial,
486	  };
487	}

490	  LifecycleScope.Agent,
491	  IAgentContextProjectorService,
492	  AgentContextProjectorService,
493	  InstantiationType.Delayed,
494	  'contextProjector',
495	);

1	/**
2	 * `spine` domain (L4) — `IAgentSpineService` implementation.
3	 *
4	 * Owns the model-driven task tree in the wire `SpineModel`: the read-only /
5	 * receipt-only control tools hand validated intent here (`acceptOpen` /
6	 * `acceptClose` / `acceptNext`), which registers the single per-step pending
7	 * transition; the `loop.afterStep` hook then commits it (`spine.open` /
8	 * `spine.close` / `spine.next`) once the matching assistant tool-call and tool
9	 * result have both landed in `contextMemory`, so the tree moves only on
10	 * observed evidence. Reads the cursor and node layout through
11	 * `wire.getModel(SpineModel)`, writes through `wire.dispatch(spineOpen(...))`
12	 * etc., records each node's provider-token baseline via `contextSize`,
13	 * assembles continuation memory with `spineTree.assembleMemoryBody`, and
14	 * renders the read-only `spine_tree` view. Hooks self-check the `KIMI_CODE_SPINE`
15	 * gate so a disabled spine never observes history. Bound at Agent scope.
16	 */
17	
18	import { Disposable } from '#/_base/di/lifecycle';
19	import { InstantiationType } from '#/_base/di/extensions';
20	import { LifecycleScope, registerScopedService } from '#/_base/di/scope';
21	import { IAgentContextMemoryService } from '#/agent/contextMemory/contextMemory';
22	import type { ContextMessage } from '#/agent/contextMemory/types';
23	import { IAgentContextSizeService } from '#/agent/contextSize/contextSize';
24	import { IAgentLoopService } from '#/agent/loop/loop';
25	import { IAgentProfileService } from '#/agent/profile/profile';
26	import { IAgentScopeContext } from '#/agent/scopeContext/scopeContext';
27	import { IHostEnvironment } from '#/os/interface/hostEnvironment';
28	import { IHostFileSystem } from '#/os/interface/hostFileSystem';
29	import { ISessionWorkspaceContext } from '#/session/workspaceContext/workspaceContext';
30	import { IAgentWireService } from '#/wire/tokens';
31	import type { IWireService } from '#/wire/wireService';
32	
33	import { isSpineEnabled } from './flag';
34	import { loadSpineViewOverride } from './instructions';
35	import {
36	  IAgentSpineService,
37	  type SpineTransitionResult,
38	} from './spine';
39	import { buildArchiveContent, spineArchivePath, writeNodeArchive } from './spineArchive';
40	import { foldSpine, type SpineFoldStatus } from './spineFold';
41	import {
42	  SpineModel,
43	  spineClose,
44	  spineNext,
45	  spineOpen,
46	  type SpineNode,
47	  type SpineState,
48	} from './spineOps';
49	import {
50	  assembleMemoryBody,
51	  childNodeId,
52	  isRootEpoch,
53	  nextChildIndex,
54	  parentNodeId,
55	  renderTree,
56	  type SpineTreeNodeView,
57	} from './spineTree';
58	
59	type SpinePending =
60	  | { readonly kind: 'open'; readonly toolCallId: string; readonly summary: string }
61	  | { readonly kind: 'close'; readonly toolCallId: string; readonly memory: string }
62	  | {
63	      readonly kind: 'next';
64	      readonly toolCallId: string;
65	      readonly summary: string;
66	      readonly memory: string;
67	    };
68	
69	const REJECT_DISABLED: SpineTransitionResult = {
70	  accepted: false,
71	  reason: 'Spine is disabled. Set KIMI_CODE_SPINE=1 to enable it.',
72	};
73	
74	const REJECT_CONFLICT: SpineTransitionResult = {
75	  accepted: false,
76	  reason:
77	    'A single assistant response may include at most one Spine transition (open, close, or next).',
78	};
79	
80	const REJECT_ROOT_EPOCH: SpineTransitionResult = {
81	  accepted: false,
82	  reason:
83	    'Root-epoch nodes cannot be closed. Use open to start a child node under the current scope.',
84	};
85	
86	export class AgentSpineService extends Disposable implements IAgentSpineService {
87	  declare readonly _serviceBrand: undefined;
88	
89	  private pending: SpinePending | null = null;
90	  private lastObservedIndex = 0;
91	
92	  constructor(
93	    @IAgentContextMemoryService private readonly context: IAgentContextMemoryService,
94	    @IAgentContextSizeService private readonly contextSize: IAgentContextSizeService,
95	    @IAgentProfileService private readonly profile: IAgentProfileService,
96	    @IHostFileSystem private readonly hostFs: IHostFileSystem,
97	    @IHostEnvironment private readonly hostEnv: IHostEnvironment,
98	    @ISessionWorkspaceContext private readonly workspace: ISessionWorkspaceContext,
99	    @IAgentScopeContext private readonly agentScope: IAgentScopeContext,
100	    @IAgentWireService private readonly wire: IWireService,
101	    @IAgentLoopService loop: IAgentLoopService,
102	  ) {
103	    super();
104	    if (this.enabled) {
105	      void loadSpineViewOverride(this.hostFs, this.hostEnv.homeDir);
106	    }
107	    this._register(
108	      loop.hooks.beforeStep.register('spine', async (_ctx, next) => {
109	        this.pending = null;
110	        await next();
111	      }),
112	    );
113	    this._register(
114	      loop.hooks.afterStep.register('spine', async (_ctx, next) => {
115	        await this.commitPending();
116	        await next();
117	      }),
118	    );
119	    this._register(
120	      this.wire.onRestored(() => {
121	        this.lastObservedIndex = this.context.get().length;
122	        this.pending = null;
123	      }),
124	    );
125	  }
126	
127	  get enabled(): boolean {
128	    return isSpineEnabled();
129	  }
130	
131	  acceptOpen(summary: string, toolCallId: string): SpineTransitionResult {
132	    const guard = this.guard();
133	    if (guard !== null) return guard;
134	    const trimmed = summary.trim();
135	    if (trimmed.length === 0) return reject('open summary must not be empty.');
136	    this.pending = { kind: 'open', toolCallId, summary: trimmed };
137	    return { accepted: true };
138	  }
139	
140	  acceptClose(memory: string, toolCallId: string): SpineTransitionResult {
141	    const guard = this.guard();
142	    if (guard !== null) return guard;
143	    const trimmed = memory.trim();
144	    if (trimmed.length === 0) return reject('close memory must not be empty.');
145	    if (isRootEpoch(this.cursorId())) return REJECT_ROOT_EPOCH;
146	    this.pending = { kind: 'close', toolCallId, memory: trimmed };
147	    return { accepted: true };
148	  }
149	
150	  acceptNext(summary: string, memory: string, toolCallId: string): SpineTransitionResult {
151	    const guard = this.guard();
152	    if (guard !== null) return guard;
153	    const trimmedSummary = summary.trim();
154	    const trimmedMemory = memory.trim();
155	    if (trimmedSummary.length === 0) return reject('next summary must not be empty.');
156	    if (trimmedMemory.length === 0) return reject('next memory must not be empty.');
157	    if (isRootEpoch(this.cursorId())) return REJECT_ROOT_EPOCH;
158	    this.pending = { kind: 'next', toolCallId, summary: trimmedSummary, memory: trimmedMemory };
159	    return { accepted: true };
160	  }
161	
162	  renderTree(): string {
163	    const state = this.state();
164	    const rootId = String(state.rootEpoch);
165	    return renderTree({
166	      cursorId: this.cursorId(),
167	      rootIds: state.nodes[rootId] === undefined ? [] : [rootId],
168	      resolve: (id) => this.nodeView(state, id),
169	    });
170	  }
171	
172	  fold(messages: readonly ContextMessage[]): readonly ContextMessage[] {
173	    if (!this.enabled) return messages;
174	    const state = this.state();
175	    const epochSummaryMessage =
176	      state.epochMemoryAt === undefined ? undefined : messages[state.epochMemoryAt];
177	    return foldSpine(messages, { state, status: this.buildStatus(), epochSummaryMessage });
178	  }
179	
180	  private buildStatus(): SpineFoldStatus {
181	    const state = this.state();
182	    const cursorId = topOf(state);
183	    const cursor = state.nodes[cursorId];
184	    const summary = cursor?.summary ?? '';
185	    const openedAt = cursor === undefined ? 0 : Math.max(0, cursor.openedAt);
186	    const maxContextTokens = this.profile.getModelCapabilities().max_context_tokens;
187	    const used = this.contextSize.get().size;
188	    const contextLeft =
189	      maxContextTokens !== undefined && maxContextTokens > 0
190	        ? Math.max(0, maxContextTokens - used)
191	        : undefined;
192	    return {
193	      cursorId,
194	      summary,
195	      parentId: parentNodeId(cursorId),
196	      cursorContext: this.contextSize.get(openedAt).size,
197	      contextLeft,
198	    };
199	  }
200	
201	  private guard(): SpineTransitionResult | null {
202	    if (!this.enabled) return REJECT_DISABLED;
203	    if (this.pending !== null) return REJECT_CONFLICT;
204	    return null;
205	  }
206	
207	  private state(): SpineState {
208	    return this.wire.getModel(SpineModel);
209	  }
210	
211	  private cursorId(): string {
212	    const stack = this.state().openStack;
213	    const top = stack.at(-1);
214	    if (top === undefined) {
215	      throw new Error('Spine openStack is empty; the tree must always contain a root epoch.');
216	    }
217	    return top;
218	  }
219	
220	  private nodeView(state: SpineState, id: string): SpineTreeNodeView | undefined {
221	    const node = state.nodes[id];
222	    if (node === undefined) return undefined;
223	    return {
224	      id: node.id,
225	      summary: node.summary,
226	      closed: node.closedAt !== undefined,
227	      archivePath: node.archivePath,
228	      tokenCost: undefined,
229	      children: node.children
230	        .map((childId) => this.nodeView(state, childId))
231	        .filter((child): child is SpineTreeNodeView => child !== undefined),
232	    };
233	  }
234	
235	  private async commitPending(): Promise<void> {
236	    if (!this.enabled) return;
237	    const pending = this.pending;
238	    if (pending === null) return;
239	
240	    const history = this.context.get();
241	    const evidence = findEvidence(history, this.lastObservedIndex, pending.toolCallId);
242	    this.lastObservedIndex = history.length;
243	    if (evidence === null) return;
244	
245	    switch (pending.kind) {
246	      case 'open':
247	        this.commitOpen(pending.summary, evidence.assistantIndex);
248	        break;
249	      case 'close':
250	        await this.commitClose(pending.memory, evidence.toolResultIndex);
251	        break;
252	      case 'next':
253	        await this.commitNext(pending.summary, pending.memory, evidence.toolResultIndex);
254	        break;
255	    }
256	    this.pending = null;
257	  }
258	
259	  private commitOpen(summary: string, openedAt: number): void {
260	    const state = this.state();
261	    const parentId = topOf(state);
262	    const parent = state.nodes[parentId];
263	    if (parent === undefined) return;
264	    const id = childNodeId(parentId, nextChildIndex(parent.children));
265	    this.wire.dispatch(
266	      spineOpen({
267	        id,
268	        summary,
269	        parentId,
270	        openedAt,
271	        baselineTokens: this.contextSize.get().size,
272	      }),
273	    );
274	  }
275	
276	  private async commitClose(memory: string, closedAt: number): Promise<void> {
277	    const state = this.state();
278	    const id = topOf(state);
279	    const node = state.nodes[id];
280	    if (node === undefined || isRootEpoch(id)) return;
281	    const assembled = assembleMemoryBody({
282	      childMemories: closedChildMemories(state, node),
283	      nodeMemory: memory,
284	    });
285	    const closing: SpineNode = { ...node, closedAt, memory: assembled };
286	    const archivePath = await this.archiveNode(closing);
287	    this.wire.dispatch(spineClose({ id, closedAt, memory: assembled, archivePath }));
288	  }
289	
290	  private async commitNext(summary: string, memory: string, closedAt: number): Promise<void> {
291	    const state = this.state();
292	    const closedId = topOf(state);
293	    const closing = state.nodes[closedId];
294	    if (closing === undefined || isRootEpoch(closedId)) return;
295	    const parentId = parentNodeId(closedId);
296	    if (parentId === null) return;
297	    const parent = state.nodes[parentId];
298	    if (parent === undefined) return;
299	    const openedId = childNodeId(parentId, nextChildIndex(parent.children));
300	    const assembled = assembleMemoryBody({
301	      childMemories: closedChildMemories(state, closing),
302	      nodeMemory: memory,
303	    });
304	    const closed: SpineNode = { ...closing, closedAt, memory: assembled };
305	    const archivePath = await this.archiveNode(closed);
306	    this.wire.dispatch(
307	      spineNext({
308	        closedId,
309	        closedAt,
310	        memory: assembled,
311	        archivePath,
312	        openedId,
313	        summary,
314	        baselineTokens: this.contextSize.get().size,
315	      }),
316	    );
317	  }
318	
319	  private async archiveNode(node: SpineNode): Promise<string | undefined> {
320	    const path = spineArchivePath(this.workspace.workDir, this.agentScope.agentId, node.id);
321	    const openedAt = Math.max(0, node.openedAt);
322	    const closedAt = node.closedAt ?? node.openedAt;
323	    const messages = this.context.get().slice(openedAt, closedAt + 1);
324	    const content = buildArchiveContent({ node, messages });
325	    try {
326	      await writeNodeArchive(this.hostFs, path, content);
327	      return path;
328	    } catch {
329	      return undefined;
330	    }
331	  }
332	}
333	
334	function topOf(state: SpineState): string {
335	  const top = state.openStack.at(-1);
336	  if (top === undefined) {
337	    throw new Error('Spine openStack is empty; the tree must always contain a root epoch.');
338	  }
339	  return top;
340	}
341	
342	function closedChildMemories(state: SpineState, node: SpineNode): readonly string[] {
343	  const bodies: string[] = [];
344	  for (const childId of node.children) {
345	    const child = state.nodes[childId];
346	    if (child !== undefined && child.closedAt !== undefined && child.memory !== undefined) {
347	      bodies.push(child.memory);
348	    }
349	  }
350	  return bodies;
351	}
352	
353	interface SpineEvidence {
354	  readonly assistantIndex: number;
355	  readonly toolResultIndex: number;
356	}
357	
358	function findEvidence(
359	  history: readonly ContextMessage[],
360	  from: number,
361	  toolCallId: string,
362	): SpineEvidence | null {
363	  let assistantIndex = -1;
364	  for (let i = from; i < history.length; i++) {
365	    const message = history[i];
366	    if (message === undefined) continue;
367	    if (
368	      assistantIndex < 0 &&
369	      message.role === 'assistant' &&
370	      message.toolCalls.some((call) => call.id === toolCallId)
371	    ) {
372	      assistantIndex = i;
373	    }
374	    if (message.role === 'tool' && message.toolCallId === toolCallId) {
375	      return assistantIndex < 0 ? null : { assistantIndex, toolResultIndex: i };
376	    }
377	  }
378	  return null;
379	}
380	
381	function reject(reason: string): SpineTransitionResult {
382	  return { accepted: false, reason };
383	}
384	
385	registerScopedService(
386	  LifecycleScope.Agent,
387	  IAgentSpineService,
388	  AgentSpineService,
389	  InstantiationType.Eager,
390	  'spine',
391	);

1	/**
2	 * `spine` domain (L4) — wire Model (`SpineModel`) and the Ops that mutate the
3	 * model-driven task tree (`spine.open` / `spine.close` / `spine.next` /
4	 * `spine.root_compact`).
5	 *
6	 * Declares the tree as `SpineState` (initial root epoch `1` with an open
7	 * synthetic startup node `1.1`): a node map, the open-node stack (its top is
8	 * the cursor), and the current root-epoch boundary. Every Op's `apply` is a
9	 * pure state transform that returns a NEW reference on a real change and the
10	 * SAME reference when its guard fails (so the wire's reference-equality gate
11	 * stays quiet); guards reject malformed or out-of-order payloads so a replay
12	 * never lands the tree in an inconsistent shape — cursor, parent linkage and
13	 * child numbering are derived by the live service, never trusted from a record.
14	 * Node ids and memory bodies live on the records themselves, so `wire.dispatch`
15	 * and `wire.replay` rebuild the same tree. Consumed by the Agent-scope
16	 * `spineService` and the `spineFold` projection.
17	 */
18	
19	import { defineModel } from '#/wire/model';
20	import { defineOp } from '#/wire/op';
21	
22	import {
23	  epochStartupNodeId,
24	  isRootEpoch,
25	  SPINE_STARTUP_OPENED_AT,
26	} from './spineTree';
27	
28	export interface SpineNode {
29	  readonly id: string;
30	  readonly summary: string;
31	  readonly openedAt: number;
32	  readonly closedAt?: number;
33	  readonly memory?: string;
34	  readonly archivePath?: string;
35	  readonly baselineTokens?: number;
36	  readonly children: readonly string[];
37	}
38	
39	export interface SpineState {
40	  readonly nodes: Readonly<Record<string, SpineNode>>;
41	  readonly openStack: readonly string[];
42	  readonly rootEpoch: number;
43	  readonly epochStartAt: number;
44	  readonly epochMemoryAt?: number;
45	}
46	
47	function syntheticEpochNode(epoch: number): SpineNode {
48	  return {
49	    id: String(epoch),
50	    summary: `root epoch ${String(epoch)}`,
51	    openedAt: SPINE_STARTUP_OPENED_AT,
52	    children: [epochStartupNodeId(epoch)],
53	  };
54	}
55	
56	function syntheticStartupNode(epoch: number): SpineNode {
57	  return {
58	    id: epochStartupNodeId(epoch),
59	    summary: 'startup',
60	    openedAt: SPINE_STARTUP_OPENED_AT,
61	    children: [],
62	  };
63	}
64	
65	function initialSpineState(): SpineState {
66	  const epoch = syntheticEpochNode(1);
67	  const startup = syntheticStartupNode(1);
68	  return {
69	    nodes: { [epoch.id]: epoch, [startup.id]: startup },
70	    openStack: [epoch.id, startup.id],
71	    rootEpoch: 1,
72	    epochStartAt: 0,
73	  };
74	}
75	
76	export const SpineModel = defineModel<SpineState>('spine', initialSpineState);
77	
78	export interface SpineOpenPayload {
79	  readonly id: string;
80	  readonly summary: string;
81	  readonly parentId: string;
82	  readonly openedAt: number;
83	  readonly baselineTokens?: number;
84	}
85	
86	export const spineOpen = defineOp(SpineModel, 'spine.open', {
87	  apply: (s, p: SpineOpenPayload): SpineState => {
88	    const parent = s.nodes[p.parentId];
89	    const top = s.openStack.at(-1);
90	    if (parent === undefined || parent.closedAt !== undefined) return s;
91	    if (top !== p.parentId || p.id in s.nodes || p.summary.length === 0) return s;
92	    const node: SpineNode = {
93	      id: p.id,
94	      summary: p.summary,
95	      openedAt: p.openedAt,
96	      baselineTokens: p.baselineTokens,
97	      children: [],
98	    };
99	    return {
100	      ...s,
101	      nodes: {
102	        ...s.nodes,
103	        [p.id]: node,
104	        [p.parentId]: { ...parent, children: [...parent.children, p.id] },
105	      },
106	      openStack: [...s.openStack, p.id],
107	    };
108	  },
109	});
110	
111	export interface SpineClosePayload {
112	  readonly id: string;
113	  readonly closedAt: number;
114	  readonly memory: string;
115	  readonly archivePath?: string;
116	}
117	
118	export const spineClose = defineOp(SpineModel, 'spine.close', {
119	  apply: (s, p: SpineClosePayload): SpineState => {
120	    const node = s.nodes[p.id];
121	    const top = s.openStack.at(-1);
122	    if (node === undefined || node.closedAt !== undefined) return s;
123	    if (top !== p.id || isRootEpoch(p.id) || p.memory.length === 0) return s;
124	    return {
125	      ...s,
126	      nodes: {
127	        ...s.nodes,
128	        [p.id]: {
129	          ...node,
130	          closedAt: p.closedAt,
131	          memory: p.memory,
132	          archivePath: p.archivePath,
133	        },
134	      },
135	      openStack: s.openStack.slice(0, -1),
136	    };
137	  },
138	});
139	
140	export interface SpineNextPayload {
141	  readonly closedId: string;
142	  readonly closedAt: number;
143	  readonly memory: string;
144	  readonly archivePath?: string;
145	  readonly openedId: string;
146	  readonly summary: string;
147	  readonly baselineTokens?: number;
148	}
149	
150	export const spineNext = defineOp(SpineModel, 'spine.next', {
151	  apply: (s, p: SpineNextPayload): SpineState => {
152	    const closing = s.nodes[p.closedId];
153	    const top = s.openStack.at(-1);
154	    if (closing === undefined || closing.closedAt !== undefined) return s;
155	    if (top !== p.closedId || isRootEpoch(p.closedId) || p.memory.length === 0) return s;
156	    if (p.openedId in s.nodes || p.summary.length === 0) return s;
157	    const parentStack = s.openStack.slice(0, -1);
158	    const parentId = parentStack.at(-1);
159	    if (parentId === undefined) return s;
160	    const parent = s.nodes[parentId];
161	    if (parent === undefined) return s;
162	    const opened: SpineNode = {
163	      id: p.openedId,
164	      summary: p.summary,
165	      openedAt: p.closedAt,
166	      baselineTokens: p.baselineTokens,
167	      children: [],
168	    };
169	    return {
170	      ...s,
171	      nodes: {
172	        ...s.nodes,
173	        [p.closedId]: {
174	          ...closing,
175	          closedAt: p.closedAt,
176	          memory: p.memory,
177	          archivePath: p.archivePath,
178	        },
179	        [p.openedId]: opened,
180	        [parentId]: { ...parent, children: [...parent.children, p.openedId] },
181	      },
182	      openStack: [...parentStack, p.openedId],
183	    };
184	  },
185	});
186	
187	export interface SpineRootCompactPayload {
188	  readonly epoch: number;
189	  readonly epochStartAt: number;
190	  readonly epochMemoryAt: number;
191	}
192	
193	export const spineRootCompact = defineOp(SpineModel, 'spine.root_compact', {
194	  apply: (s, p: SpineRootCompactPayload): SpineState => {
195	    if (p.epoch !== s.rootEpoch + 1) return s;
196	    if (p.epochStartAt < 0) return s;
197	    const epoch = syntheticEpochNode(p.epoch);
198	    const startup = syntheticStartupNode(p.epoch);
199	    return {
200	      nodes: { ...s.nodes, [epoch.id]: epoch, [startup.id]: startup },
201	      openStack: [epoch.id, startup.id],
202	      rootEpoch: p.epoch,
203	      epochStartAt: p.epochStartAt,
204	      epochMemoryAt: p.epochMemoryAt,
205	    };
206	  },
207	});
208

213	    'spine.next': SpineNextPayload;
214	    'spine.root_compact': SpineRootCompactPayload;
215	  }
