65	export class AgentContextMemoryService extends Disposable implements IAgentContextMemoryService {
66	  declare readonly _serviceBrand: undefined;
67	
68	  constructor(
69	    @IAgentWireService private readonly wire: IWireService,
70	    @IEventBus private readonly eventBus: IEventBus,
71	  ) {
72	    super();
73	  }
74	
75	  get(): readonly ContextMessage[] {
76	    return this.wire.getModel(ContextModel) as readonly ContextMessage[];
77	  }
78	
79	  append(...messages: readonly ContextMessage[]): void {
80	    if (messages.length === 0) return;
81	    const start = this.get().length;
82	    this.wire.dispatch(...messages.map((message) => contextAppendMessage({ message })));
83	    this.publishSplice({ start, deleteCount: 0, messages: [...messages] });
84	  }
85	
86	  appendLoopEvent(event: LoopRecordedEvent): void {
87	    this.wire.dispatch(contextAppendLoopEvent({ event }));
88	  }
89	  clear(): void {
90	    const deleteCount = this.get().length;
91	    if (deleteCount === 0) return;
92	    this.wire.dispatch(contextClear({}), contextSizeMeasured({ length: 0, tokens: 0 }));
93	    this.publishSplice({ start: 0, deleteCount, messages: [] });
94	  }
95	
96	  undo(count: number): UndoCut {
97	    const history = this.get();
98	    const cut = computeUndoCut(history, count);
99	    if (isFullyUndoable(cut, count)) {
100	      this.wire.dispatch(contextUndo({ count }), ...this.sizeOpsForCut(cut.cutIndex, history));
101	      this.publishSplice({
102	        start: cut.cutIndex,
103	        deleteCount: history.length - cut.cutIndex,
104	        messages: [],
105	      });
106	    }
107	    return cut;
108	  }
109	
110	  applyCompaction(input: ContextCompactionInput): ContextCompactionResult {
111	    const history = this.get();
112	    const result = buildContextCompactionShape(history, input);
113	    this.wire.dispatch(
114	      contextApplyCompaction({
115	        summary: result.summary,
116	        contextSummary: result.contextSummary,
117	        compactedCount: result.compactedCount,
118	        tokensBefore: result.tokensBefore,
119	        tokensAfter: result.tokensAfter,
120	        keptUserMessageCount: result.keptUserMessageCount,
121	        keptHeadUserMessageCount: result.keptHeadUserMessageCount,
122	        droppedCount: result.droppedCount,
123	      }),
124	      contextSizeMeasured({ length: result.messages.length, tokens: result.tokensAfter }),
125	    );
126	    this.publishSplice({
127	      start: 0,
128	      deleteCount: history.length,
129	      messages: [...result.messages],
130	      tokens: result.tokensAfter,
131	    });
132	    const { messages: _messages, ...publicResult } = result;
133	    void _messages;
134	    return publicResult;
135	  }
136	
137	  private publishSplice(input: {
138	    start: number;
139	    deleteCount: number;
140	    messages: readonly ContextMessage[];
141	    tokens?: number;
142	  }): void {
143	    this.eventBus.publish({ type: 'context.spliced', ...input });
144	  }
145	
146	  /**
147	   * Cascade a `context_size.measured` Op when an undo truncates the measured
148	   * prefix (`ContextSizeModel.length`). If the surviving context still covers
149	   * the measured prefix, the measurement stays valid and nothing is emitted;
150	   * otherwise the prefix is rebased to an estimate of the surviving messages
151	   * (an aggregate measured count can't be truncated without per-message data).
152	   */
153	  private sizeOpsForCut(cutIndex: number, history: readonly ContextMessage[]): Op[] {
154	    const model = this.wire.getModel(ContextSizeModel);
155	    if (model.length <= cutIndex) return [];
156	    return [
157	      contextSizeMeasured({
158	        length: cutIndex,
159	        tokens: estimateTokensForMessages(history.slice(0, cutIndex)),
160	      }),
161	    ];
162	  }
163	}

1	/**
2	 * `llmRequester` domain (L3) — `IAgentLLMRequesterService` implementation.
3	 *
4	 * Thin shell over the god-object `Model` (App scope). Assembles per-turn
5	 * `LLMRequestInput` from `profile` (system prompt), `contextMemory` +
6	 * `contextProjector` (history), and `toolRegistry` (tools), applies the
7	 * completion-token budget, then drives `model.request(input, signal)` with
8	 * bounded retry. Forwards streamed `part` events to the caller's `onPart`
9	 * handler, records `usage` through `IAgentUsageService`, resolves to an
10	 * `LLMRequestFinish` on the `finish` event, logs the request lifecycle
11	 * (config deduplicated by content, request/response/failure lines, plus
12	 * per-request fields) through `log`, records durable request-trace Ops
13	 * through `wire`, and reports provider failures through `telemetry`. Bound
14	 * at Agent scope.
15	 */

109	export class AgentLLMRequesterService implements IAgentLLMRequesterService {

114	  constructor(
115	    @IAgentContextMemoryService private readonly context: IAgentContextMemoryService,
116	    @IAgentContextProjectorService private readonly projector: IAgentContextProjectorService,
117	    @IAgentContextSizeService private readonly contextSize: IAgentContextSizeService,

125	  ) {}
126	
127	  async request(
128	    overrides: LLMRequestOverrides = {},
129	    onPart: LLMRequestPartHandler = noopOnPart,
130	    signal?: AbortSignal,
131	  ): Promise<LLMRequestFinish> {
132	    signal?.throwIfAborted();
133	    return this.requestWithRetry(overrides, onPart, signal);
134	  }
135	
136	  private async requestWithRetry(
137	    overrides: LLMRequestOverrides,
138	    onPart: LLMRequestPartHandler,
139	    signal: AbortSignal | undefined,
140	  ): Promise<LLMRequestFinish> {
141	    const startedAt = Date.now();
142	    const maxAttempts = Math.max(overrides.retry?.maxAttempts ?? DEFAULT_MAX_RETRY_ATTEMPTS, 1);
143	
144	    if (maxAttempts <= 1) {
145	      try {
146	        return await this.executeRequestAttempt(overrides, onPart, signal, 1, maxAttempts);
147	      } catch (error) {
148	        this.logRequestFailure(error, overrides, signal, 1, maxAttempts);
149	        this.trackApiError(error, startedAt, signal);
150	        throw error;
151	      }
152	    }
153	
154	    const delays = retryBackoffDelays(maxAttempts);
155	    for (let attempt = 1; ; attempt += 1) {
156	      try {
157	        return await this.executeRequestAttempt(overrides, onPart, signal, attempt, maxAttempts);
158	      } catch (error) {
159	        if (attempt >= maxAttempts || !isRetryableGenerateError(error)) {
160	          this.logRequestFailure(error, overrides, signal, attempt, maxAttempts);
161	          this.trackApiError(error, startedAt, signal);
162	          throw error;
163	        }
164	
165	        signal?.throwIfAborted();
166	        const delayMs = delays[attempt - 1] ?? 0;
167	        await overrides.retry?.onRetry?.({
168	          failedAttempt: attempt,
169	          nextAttempt: attempt + 1,
170	          maxAttempts,
171	          delayMs,
172	          ...retryErrorFields(error),
173	        });
174	        await sleepForRetry(delayMs, signal);
175	      }
176	    }
177	  }
178	
179	  private async executeRequestAttempt(
180	    overrides: LLMRequestOverrides,
181	    onPart: LLMRequestPartHandler,
182	    signal: AbortSignal | undefined,
183	    attempt: number,
184	    maxAttempts: number,
185	  ): Promise<LLMRequestFinish> {
186	    signal?.throwIfAborted();
187	    const request = this.resolveRequest(
188	      overrides,
189	      attempt === 1 ? undefined : { attempt: `${String(attempt)}/${String(maxAttempts)}` },
190	    );
191	    return this.runRequest(request, onPart, signal);
192	  }
193	
194	  private logRequestFailure(
195	    error: unknown,
196	    overrides: LLMRequestOverrides,
197	    signal: AbortSignal | undefined,
198	    attempt: number,
199	    maxAttempts: number,
200	  ): void {
201	    if (isAbortError(error) || signal?.aborted === true) return;
202	    const payload: LogContext = {
203	      ...logFieldsForSource(overrides.source),
204	      attempt: `${String(attempt)}/${String(maxAttempts)}`,
205	      model: this.profile.data().modelAlias ?? 'unknown',
206	      ...retryErrorFields(error),
207	    };
208	    this.log.warn('llm request failed', payload);
209	  }
210	
211	  private trackApiError(
212	    error: unknown,
213	    startedAt: number,
214	    signal: AbortSignal | undefined,
215	  ): void {
216	    if (isAbortError(error) || signal?.aborted === true) return;
217	    const properties: Record<string, unknown> = {
218	      error_type: apiErrorType(error),
219	      model: this.profile.data().modelAlias ?? 'unknown',
220	      retryable: isRetryableGenerateError(error),
221	      duration_ms: Math.max(0, Date.now() - startedAt),
222	    };
223	    const statusCode = apiStatusCode(error);
224	    if (statusCode !== undefined) properties['status_code'] = statusCode;
225	    this.telemetry.track('api_error', properties);
226	  }
227	
228	  private async runRequest(
229	    request: ResolvedLLMRequest,
230	    onPart: LLMRequestPartHandler,
231	    signal: AbortSignal | undefined,
232	  ): Promise<LLMRequestFinish> {
233	    const requestInput = (strict: boolean) => ({
234	      systemPrompt: request.systemPrompt,
235	      tools: request.tools,
236	      messages: strict
237	        ? this.projector.projectStrict(request.messages)
238	        : this.projector.project(request.messages),
239	    });

```ts
get(start?, end?): ContextSize {
  const context = this.context.get();
  const model = this.wire.getModel(ContextSizeModel);
  const from = normalizeSliceIndex(start ?? 0, context.length);
  const to = normalizeSliceIndex(end ?? context.length, context.length);
  const measuredEnd = Math.min(to, model.length);
  const estimatedStart = Math.max(from, model.length);
  const measured = (from === 0 && measuredEnd === model.length)
    ? model.tokens
    : this.subRange(context, from, measuredEnd, model);
  const estimated = estimateTokensForMessages(context.slice(estimatedStart, to));
  return { size: measured + estimated, measured, estimated };
}
```

subRange via snapshot chain (request-caliber diff where possible):
```ts
// tokens of prefix [0, x) — request caliber best effort
private prefixTokens(context, x: number, model): number {
  if (x <= 0) return 0;
  if (x === model.length) return model.tokens;
  // nearest snapshot with storageLength <= x
  let best: ContextSizeSnapshot | undefined;
  for (const snap of model.snapshots) { if (snap.storageLength <= x) best = snap; else break; }
  if (best === undefined) return estimateTokensForMessages(context.slice(0, x));
  return best.tokens + estimateTokensForMessages(context.slice(best.storageLength, x));
}
subRange = prefixTokens(to') - prefixTokens(from), guarded >= 0 (monotonicity guard → fallback full estimate of slice if negative)
```

**E. Estimator calibration (Tier 1b)** `_base/utils/tokens.ts`

Calibration without a real tokenizer in-repo: best effort with char classes. Let me draft:

```ts
export function estimateTokens(text: string): number {
  let word = 0, symbol = 0, space = 0, nonAscii = 0;
  for (const char of text) {
    const cp = char.codePointAt(0)!;
    if (cp > 127) { nonAscii++; continue; }
    if (char === ' ' || char === '\t' || char === '\n' || char === '\r') { space++; continue; }
    if ((cp >= 48 && cp <= 57) || (cp >= 65 && cp <= 90) || (cp >= 97 && cp <= 122) || char === '_') { word++; continue; }
    symbol++;
  }
  return Math.ceil(word / 3.5 + symbol / 2.5 + space / 8) + nonAscii;
}
```

Hmm, is this materially better than ascii/4? For `{"type":"tool_result","content":[...]}` style JSON: ratio ~2.8-3.2 → word/3.5 + sym/2.5 ≈ better than /4. For prose: ~4 → word/3.5 overestimates prose ~14%. For code: variable names words, plus symbols → ~3-ish better.

50	  it('replaces a closed node span with one memory message without mutating storage', () => {

161	function fold(ctx: TestAgentContext): readonly ContextMessage[] {
162	  return ctx.get(IAgentSpineService).fold(ctx.context.get()) as readonly ContextMessage[];
163	}
164	
165	function wireNode(
166	  ctx: TestAgentContext,
167	  input: { id: string; openedAt: number; closedAt: number; memory: string },
168	): void {
169	  const wire = ctx.get(IAgentWireService);
170	  wire.dispatch(spineOpen({ id: input.id, summary: 'task A', parentId: '1.1', openedAt: input.openedAt }));
171	  wire.dispatch(spineClose({ id: input.id, closedAt: input.closedAt, memory: input.memory }));
172	}
173	
174	interface ClosedNodeIndices {
175	  readonly openCall: number;
176	  readonly closeResult: number;
177	  readonly after: number;
178	}
179	
180	function buildClosedNodeHistory(ctx: TestAgentContext): ClosedNodeIndices {
181	  append(ctx, userMessage('start'));
182	  const openCall = append(ctx, assistantToolCall('c_open', 'spine_open'));
183	  append(ctx, toolResult('c_open'));
184	  append(ctx, assistantText('working'));
185	  append(ctx, assistantToolCall('c_close', 'spine_close'));
186	  const closeResult = append(ctx, toolResult('c_close'));
187	  const after = append(ctx, userMessage('after'));
188	  return { openCall, closeResult, after };
189	}
190	
191	interface NestedClosedIndices {
192	  readonly parentOpen: number;
193	  readonly childOpen: number;
194	  readonly childClose: number;
195	  readonly parentClose: number;
196	}
197	
198	function buildNestedClosedHistory(ctx: TestAgentContext): NestedClosedIndices {
199	  append(ctx, userMessage('start'));
200	  const parentOpen = append(ctx, assistantToolCall('c_parent_open', 'spine_open'));
201	  append(ctx, toolResult('c_parent_open'));
202	  const childOpen = append(ctx, assistantToolCall('c_child_open', 'spine_open'));
203	  append(ctx, toolResult('c_child_open'));
204	  append(ctx, assistantToolCall('c_child_close', 'spine_close'));
205	  const childClose = append(ctx, toolResult('c_child_close'));
206	  append(ctx, assistantToolCall('c_parent_close', 'spine_close'));
207	  const parentClose = append(ctx, toolResult('c_parent_close'));
208	  return { parentOpen, childOpen, childClose, parentClose };
209	}
210	
211	async function configureLoop(ctx: TestAgentContext): Promise<void> {
212	  ctx.configure({ provider: CATALOGUED_PROVIDER, modelCapabilities: CATALOGUED_MODEL_CAPABILITIES });
213	  await ctx.rpc.setPermission({ mode: 'yolo' });
214	}
215	
216	function append(ctx: TestAgentContext, message: ContextMessage): number {
217	  const index = ctx.context.get().length;
218	  ctx.context.append(message);
219	  return index;
220	}
221	
222	function userMessage(text: string): ContextMessage {
223	  return {
224	    role: 'user',
225	    content: [{ type: 'text', text }],
226	    toolCalls: [],
227	    origin: { kind: 'user' },
228	  };
229	}
230	
231	function assistantText(text: string): ContextMessage {
232	  return { role: 'assistant', content: [{ type: 'text', text }], toolCalls: [] };
233	}
234	
235	function assistantToolCall(id: string, name: string): ContextMessage {
236	  return {
237	    role: 'assistant',
238	    content: [{ type: 'text', text: `calling ${name}` }],
239	    toolCalls: [{ type: 'function', id, name, arguments: '{}' }],
240	  };
241	}
242	
243	function toolResult(toolCallId: string): ContextMessage {
244	  return {
245	    role: 'tool',
246	    content: [{ type: 'text', text: `result of ${toolCallId}` }],
247	    toolCalls: [],
248	    toolCallId,
249	  };
250	}
251	
252	function textOf(message: ContextMessage | undefined): string {
253	  return (
254	    message?.content?.map((part) => (part.type === 'text' ? (part.text ?? '') : '')).join('') ?? ''
255	  );
256	}

spine/main/1-1-2.md:49:A1. Write usage against the sent view as its own entity: ContextSizeModel stays "storage history"; usage reflects "sent view". Mismatch is structural: fold adds/removes content (spine_status injected, memory/compaction summaries replace messages). Any attribution back to storage indices requires knowing the fold mapping. If foldSpine produced an index map (sent message i ↔ storage message j or "synthetic"), we could distribute usage. Actually what do we need from usage? Two numbers: total context size (for context_left) and sub-interval sizes (cursor_context). Key insight: provider usage already gives exact full-prefix total → context_left could be exact directly from usage of the last request, without storing into the model at all! context_left uses contextSize.get().size (full prefix). So: capture latest usage (input/prompt tokens) in requester, store as "last measured total" on the ContextSizeModel keyed to (messageCount, fingerprint of last message?). Then get() for full prefix returns measured total. That fixes context_left exactly.

spine/main/1-1-2.md:749:Also `contextSize.get(openedAt)` cursor_context: "context accumulated in current node". In view terms/stor terms? It's shown to the user as the node's working context size. Conceptually = storage slice of the node span (raw messages since openedAt). In normal flow within a node (no closes inside, or closes get folded later), that slice's view === storage slice (until something closes). The estimation error dominates within-node. Can we get exact subrange? Differential: usage at openedAt boundary vs now — but view also changed (status line each request; annotations shift only when new user messages arrive since anchors number user requests) — annotation is one-time per message... wait userAnchor counters recompute each fold from message 0: anchors stable for past messages. Status line changes every fold (numbers change, ±5 tokens). So usage_now − usage_at_open = tokens of new storage messages (view identity over them) + status delta. If we snapshot `contextSizeMeasured` per (length, tokens) replay-safely into the wire model, openedAt snapshot tokens vs current → exact-ish node context. The op record has length+tokens only — a single current value; to snapshot at node open, spineService could record `contextSize.get().measured` at openedAt into node state (spineOps already has openedAt; add openedTokens?). But replay-safety: node state is wire model — recording the measured value at open time is a deterministic event... measured() is dispatched by llmRequester through wire — at open time the value is known → fold-recordable. Then cursor_context = currentMeasuredViewTotal − openedMeasuredViewTotal + ... eh but view restructurings between (closes...) — inside a node, when a CHILD closes, the view replaces child span with memory stub: usage drops structurally. Delta vs opened snapshot breaks. Ugh, but still: the delta semantics is "next request marginal size of this node" — after child close, child's raw messages replaced by memory stub — the node's working context in the view truly IS smaller (stub). So view-based "cursor_context = current view total − view total at open" remains meaningful!? Except history before openedAt also may restructure (parent-level closes) shifting baseline. Messy but error stays within synthetic stubs (small).

spine/main/1-1-2.md:49:A1. Write usage against the sent view as its own entity: ContextSizeModel stays "storage history"; usage reflects "sent view". Mismatch is structural: fold adds/removes content (spine_status injected, memory/compaction summaries replace messages). Any attribution back to storage indices requires knowing the fold mapping. If foldSpine produced an index map (sent message i ↔ storage message j or "synthetic"), we could distribute usage. Actually what do we need from usage? Two numbers: total context size (for context_left) and sub-interval sizes (cursor_context). Key insight: provider usage already gives exact full-prefix total → context_left could be exact directly from usage of the last request, without storing into the model at all! context_left uses contextSize.get().size (full prefix). So: capture latest usage (input/prompt tokens) in requester, store as "last measured total" on the ContextSizeModel keyed to (messageCount, fingerprint of last message?). Then get() for full prefix returns measured total. That fixes context_left exactly.
spine/main/1-1-2.md:51:But cursor_context (sub-interval [openedAt, now)) is inherently not measurable from provider usage — provider only reports total prefix. Sub-interval always needs per-message estimates... unless we do differential measurement: usage at openedAt (total then) vs usage now (total now) — difference = exact tokens for the interval since openedAt! Since spine nodes open/close at message boundaries, we can snapshot usage totals at boundaries: cursor_context = latestUsageTotal − usageTotalAt(openedAt). That's exact (modulo fold/view differences!). Caveat: fold content at request N — the usage at boundary was measured under a possibly different view (different spine_status text, different folded memory). The spine_status block changes every request → the "prefix" isn't a shared prefix token-wise. The delta between two usage values includes deltas of the status block itself (small, bounded). Hmm — but how large is spine_status? It's the envelope with cursor/summary/context stats — maybe 50–150 tokens. The drift is small per measurement but error = difference of two bounded errors. Also compaction/folding between the two points makes views wildly different, delta meaningless. But wait — does fold happen every request regardless? <spine_status> appended every time, its text changes (context_left changes), so per-request token counts of the envelope vary. Still, attributing delta to messages: at the moment right after node opens, snapshot usage u0 at boundary message index; later usage u1 at same view family; u1−u0 ≈ tokens of new messages + envelope drift. Error maybe tens–few hundred tokens. Way better than estimation (code underestimation could be 30%+).

spine/main/1-1-2.md-45-The crux of the fix design: options
spine/main/1-1-2.md-46-
spine/main/1-1-2.md-47-Option A (cheapest, partial): relax measured() verification to a fingerprint-based approach. Instead of requiring `===` identity of entire array, compare a structural fingerprint: when sending, record for each message a cheap fingerprint (lengths/hash of content parts) keyed to storage index. Provider usage corresponds to the sent prompt tokens total. The usage count is total prompt tokens of sent view; we want to attribute tokens to stored messages. Two sub-approaches:
spine/main/1-1-2.md-48-
spine/main/1-1-2.md:49:A1. Write usage against the sent view as its own entity: ContextSizeModel stays "storage history"; usage reflects "sent view". Mismatch is structural: fold adds/removes content (spine_status injected, memory/compaction summaries replace messages). Any attribution back to storage indices requires knowing the fold mapping. If foldSpine produced an index map (sent message i ↔ storage message j or "synthetic"), we could distribute usage. Actually what do we need from usage? Two numbers: total context size (for context_left) and sub-interval sizes (cursor_context). Key insight: provider usage already gives exact full-prefix total → context_left could be exact directly from usage of the last request, without storing into the model at all! context_left uses contextSize.get().size (full prefix). So: capture latest usage (input/prompt tokens) in requester, store as "last measured total" on the ContextSizeModel keyed to (messageCount, fingerprint of last message?). Then get() for full prefix returns measured total. That fixes context_left exactly.
spine/main/1-1-2.md-50-
spine/main/1-1-2.md-51-But cursor_context (sub-interval [openedAt, now)) is inherently not measurable from provider usage — provider only reports total prefix. Sub-interval always needs per-message estimates... unless we do differential measurement: usage at openedAt (total then) vs usage now (total now) — difference = exact tokens for the interval since openedAt! Since spine nodes open/close at message boundaries, we can snapshot usage totals at boundaries: cursor_context = latestUsageTotal − usageTotalAt(openedAt). That's exact (modulo fold/view differences!). Caveat: fold content at request N — the usage at boundary was measured under a possibly different view (different spine_status text, different folded memory). The spine_status block changes every request → the "prefix" isn't a shared prefix token-wise. The delta between two usage values includes deltas of the status block itself (small, bounded). Hmm — but how large is spine_status? It's the envelope with cursor/summary/context stats — maybe 50–150 tokens. The drift is small per measurement but error = difference of two bounded errors. Also compaction/folding between the two points makes views wildly different, delta meaningless. But wait — does fold happen every request regardless? <spine_status> appended every time, its text changes (context_left changes), so per-request token counts of the envelope vary. Still, attributing delta to messages: at the moment right after node opens, snapshot usage u0 at boundary message index; later usage u1 at same view family; u1−u0 ≈ tokens of new messages + envelope drift. Error maybe tens–few hundred tokens. Way better than estimation (code underestimation could be 30%+).
spine/main/1-1-2.md-52-
spine/main/1-1-2.md:53:Option B (structural, higher cost): make fold produce an index map / view descriptor, and make ContextSizeModel track "view" rather than storage: compute measured tokens per storage message by mapping usage deltas per request step: tokens attributed to message i = usage(N) − usage(N−1) adjusted for known synthetic parts (envelope tokens can be estimated or better: compute by measuring? provider doesn't give per-part). Actually different providers: Anthropic gives prompt caching breakdown; OpenAI gives prompt_tokens only. Per-message attribution from usage deltas is the standard approach: each new turn, delta = new messages + envelope change. Attribute delta minus estimated envelope to the new messages proportionally? Getting complicated.
spine/main/1-1-2.md-57-The stats shown: cursor_context (used by users to judge node context) and context_left (used to judge remaining window). Display already rounds to K with ~. Tolerance: maybe ±5–10% is fine for UX. Current estimation: ascii/4 underestimates code by maybe 20–40% (code is ~3 chars/token for typical code, minified worse; Chinese ~1 char ≈ 0.6–1.5 tokens... For a 300K context that's huge error. Also tool results with JSON.
spine/main/1-1-2.md-58-
spine/main/1-1-2.md-59-Cheapest high-value fixes, ranked:
spine/main/1-1-2.md-60-
spine/main/1-1-2.md:61:Fix 1 (S, ~0.5 day): exact context_left. In llmRequester, after each response with usage, write measured total into ContextSizeModel as "total measured at message boundary N" — keyed by storage message count + fold generation id. ContextSize.get() (full prefix) returns it if storage length matches and no fold-structural change invalidated... but wait: view-vs-storage mismatch: usage counts tokens of sent view which includes <spine_status> envelope not in storage, and folded representations (compaction summaries ARE stored? full compaction writes summary message into storage — contextMemoryService writes estimates for it). The envelope: appended to the last user message or as separate message? Memory says "foldSpine 每次必追加 <spine_status>" — appended to messages array. If envelope is part of a message in view, usage total = storage-messages tokens + envelope tokens + possibly view transformations. So measured total ≈ storage total + envelope (≈100 tokens). Acceptable? For context_left the error would be envelope + any view-only transforms (spine memory injection replaces历史的? foldSpine turns closed nodes into memory blocks — those REPLACE history messages in the view! So view ≠ storage: closed-node messages are removed from view and replaced by <spine_memory> text. Usage then doesn't correspond to storage prefix at all — it's smaller or larger depending. So writing usage as "storage total" is wrong after any node closes...
spine/main/1-1-2.md-62-
spine/main/1-1-2.md-63-Unless: attribute per-message. Ugh, view transformation is the whole problem.
spine/main/1-1-2.md-64-
spine/main/1-1-2.md:65:Fix 2 (the right structural fix, M-L, 2–5 days + tests): make foldSpine return a view map: for each sent message, either (a) identity mapping to storage index, or (b) synthetic content (envelope, memory block, compaction summary) marked with no storage index or mapping to the range it summarizes. Then ContextSizeModel stores measured tokens per *view slot* with mapping; queries: storage sub-interval query composes from slots: identity slots → measured value; synthetic slots → estimate (they're small) or measured attributed to the range it summarizes? For cursor_context (current node since openedAt, all identity slots, no closes inside) → exact. For full prefix [0, len): sum of measured identity slots + measured/estimated synthetic ranges — close to usage-measured total anyway; or just use total usage directly. Cost drivers: spineFold lives in projector (conflicted!), llmRequester measured() rewrite, tests, conflict resolution dependency. Also multi-provider usage semantics: usage might be absent (some providers), cache tokens breakdown, etc. Keep fallback to estimate when no usage.

That's exact & useful. cursor_context stays estimated (bounded) — or differential if we also snapshot at open (error ≤ stubs + status drift, good enough).
